%let DATA = '2025-04-01' ;
%let DATA_FIM = '2025-04-30' ;
%let cd_assesoria = ('4035','4033','4061','4036','4024');

proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage MAX_CHAR_LEN=100 );
Create table BASE_CARTEIRA as select * from connection to perm
(SELECT *

    FROM   (SELECT A.*,

                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY cd_contrato,cd_assessoria ORDER BY qt_dias_atraso asc ) AS row_num

            FROM   (SELECT SAFE_CAST(cd_contrato AS INT64) as cd_contrato,
dt_criacao_base,
cd_assessoria,
nm_assessoria,
qt_dias_atraso,
vr_risco_contrato,
ds_produto_contrato,
fl_contrato_suspenso,
cd_tipo_suspensao,
 FROM  bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.carteiragoe_cobranca_fato
WHERE dt_criacao_base BETWEEN &DATA AND &DATA_FIM
AND ds_produto_contrato ='CARTAO_CL'
and cd_assessoria in &cd_assesoria)A) A

    WHERE  row_num = 1
)
;
disconnect from perm;
quit;

Create table BASE_CARTEIRA_GERAL as select * from connection to perm
(SELECT
	distinct FORMAT_TIMESTAMP('%Y%m', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()) AS anomes,
		ds_produto_contrato,
		count(cd_contrato)  as cd_contrato,
		CAST(SUM(vr_risco_contrato) AS FLOAT64) AS SALDO,
		


FROM  bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.carteiragoe_cobranca_fato
	WHERE dt_criacao_base ="2025-08-21"
	


GROUP BY ds_produto_contrato
	



)
;

LIBNAME ACris BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/cristiano';
proc sql;
    create table Tamanho_Tabelas as
    select
        memname as Tabela,
        nobs as Num_Observacoes,
        nvar as Num_Variaveis,
        filesize as Tamanho_MB
    from dictionary.tables
    where libname='ACRIS' 
    order by filesize desc;
quit;


proc print data=Tamanho_Tabelas noobs;
    title "Resumo das Tabelas na Biblioteca ACRIS";
run;

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 007 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 1035 */

/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-49GsbiCsOOl8Rr6k/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME CARTAO  BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/erocha/CARTAO/MOVTO';
libname CARD_ACR BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/ranapole/Cartoes/Relatorios_Acordos/Entrada/';
LIBNAME CARNEIRO BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro';
LIBNAME ACris BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/cristiano';
LIBNAME B_GERAL BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/malmeida';
LIBNAME PERF_CL BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/erocha/CARTAO/CL/MIS';

%LET SAFRA = '01jan2024'D;

DATA PERF_AMIGAVEL;
SET CARTAO.PERF_AMIGAVEL;
WHERE SAFRA >= &SAFRA ;

CDDIMCONTACARTAO = 1000000000*PRODUTO+NUCONTA;
IF TPCLASSE = 'F' THEN DT_ATRASO = DATEPART(DTENVIO_CYBER)-DIAS_ATRASO;
ELSE DT_ATRASO = DT_INICIO_ATRASO;
FORMAT DT_ATRASO DDMMYY10.;
where PILOTO_DIGITAL ='N' and COD_ASSESS <> 4010;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE  PERF_AMIGAVEL AS
SELECT
A.*,
	CASE
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%AVAL%' THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%BELLINATI%' THEN 'BELLIANTI'


WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_ TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CONCILIG%' THEN 'CONCILIG'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CRC%' THEN 'CRC'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%FLEX%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%LM%' THEN 'LM'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NOVA%' THEN 'LM'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NO_ACTION_BV' THEN 'DIGITAL'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'PILOTO_BOM' THEN 'DIGITAL'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%PASCHOAL%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%RN%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%SERVICES%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%TOLEDO%' THEN 'TOLEDO'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%VILELA%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
END AS ASSESSORIA_CORR
from PERF_AMIGAVEL a
order by safra, ASSESSORIA
;
QUIT;

DATA BASE_DU;
SET B_GERAL.DIAS_UTEIS
;
RUN;

%LET SAFRA = 202401;
%LET SAFRA2 = '01jan2024'D;

%let prefix = CART_COB_DIST_CL_202;
%let excluir = CART_COB_DIST_CL_202507_GCP;
%let prefix2 = RESULT_DIST_CL_202;
%let excluir2 = RESULT_DIST_CL_202507_GCP;

proc sql noprint;
    select cats("PERF_CL.", memname)
    into :lista_tabelas separated by ' '
    from dictionary.tables
    where libname = 'PERF_CL'
      and memname like "&prefix%"
      and memname ne "&excluir";
quit;

data CART_CL;
    set &lista_tabelas;
    where DATA_BASE >= &SAFRA ;
run;











proc sql noprint;
    select cats("PERF_CL.", memname)
    into :lista_tabelas2 separated by ' '
    from dictionary.tables
    where libname = 'PERF_CL'
      and memname like "&prefix2%"
      and memname ne "&excluir2";
quit;

data RESULTADO_CL;
    set &lista_tabelas2;
    where DATA_BASE >= &SAFRA AND TIPO <> 'COLCHAO';
run;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE  RESULTADO_CL AS
SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%AVAL%' THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%BELLINATI%' THEN 'BELLIANTI'


WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_ TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CONCILIG%' THEN 'CONCILIG'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CRC%' THEN 'CRC'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%FLEX%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%LM%' THEN 'LM'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NOVA%' THEN 'LM'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NO_ACTION_BV' THEN 'DIGITAL'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE 'PILOTO_BOM' THEN 'DIGITAL'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%PASCHOAL%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%RN%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%SERVICES%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%TOLEDO%' THEN 'TOLEDO'

WHEN A.ASSESSORIA LIKE '%VILELA%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
END AS ASSESSORIA_CORR
from RESULTADO_CL a
order by DATA_BASE, ASSESSORIA
;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.PAGTOS_CA AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEar(SAFRA),z4.),put(month(SAFRA),z2.)),z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          CHAVE AS CONTRATO,
          ASSESSORIA_CORR,
          MAX(DT_REGULARIZADO) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DT_REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.PERF_AMIGAVEL
      WHERE DT_REGULARIZADO NOT IS MISSING
GROUP BY SAFRA, CHAVE;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.PGTO_CL AS
   SELECT DATA_BASE AS SAFRA,
          
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(PRODUTO,Z2.),PUT(NUCONTA,Z10.)),Z12.)) AS CONTRATO,
			ASSESSORIA_CORR,
           MAX(DATA_PAGTO) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DT_REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL
      WHERE DATA_PAGTO NOT IS MISSING
	  GROUP BY DATA_BASE, PRODUTO, NUCONTA
      ;
QUIT;

DATA BASE_PGTOS_FULL;
SET
WORK.PAGTOS_CA
WORK.PGTO_CL
;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.CARTEIRA_CA AS
   SELECT
           DT_ARQUIVO AS DT_ENTRADA,
          
            (input(cats(put(year(t1.SAFRA),z4.),put(month(t1.SAFRA),z2.)),z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.TPCLASSE ='F' THEN 'FLUXO_CA'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO <= 30 THEN ' ATE 30'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN  31 AND 60  THEN '31 A 60'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN  61 AND 75  THEN '61 A 75'
            WHEN  t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN  76 AND 95  THEN '76 A 95'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA_CORR,
          t1.CHAVE AS CONTRATO,
          t1.VLR_COBRANCA
      FROM WORK.PERF_AMIGAVEL t1;
QUIT;



DATA CARTEIRA_CL;
SET
PERF_CL.CART_COB_DIST_CL_202:;
KEEP DATA_BASE CHAVE DIAS_AT ASSESSORIA	VR_SALDO_ATUAL TIPO DTENVIO;
WHERE TIPO <> 'COLCHAO' and DATA_BASE >= 202401 and ASSESSORIA IS NOT MISSING
AND DIAS_AT >= 75;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE WORK.CARTEIRA_CL AS
		SELECT
      DTENVIO AS DT_ENTRADA,
		
	DATA_BASE AS SAFRA,
	
	(CASE
		WHEN TIPO ='ENTRANTE' THEN 'FLUXO_CL'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 76 AND 95 THEN '76 A 95'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 95 AND 120 THEN '96 A 120'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 121 AND 150 THEN '121 A 150'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 151 AND 180 THEN '151 A 180'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 181 AND 210 THEN '181 A 210'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 211 AND 240 THEN '211 A 240'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 241 AND 270 THEN '241 A 270'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 271 AND 300 THEN '271 A 300'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 301 AND 330 THEN '301 A 330'
		WHEN DIAS_AT BETWEEN 331 AND 360 THEN '331 A 360'
		WHEN DIAS_AT > 360 THEN 'WO'
		ELSE 'VALIDAR'
		END)
	AS FAIXA_ATRASO,
CASE
WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%AVAL%' THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%BELLINATI%' THEN 'BELLIANTI' 

	
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_ TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CA_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_BELLINATI_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE 'CARTAO_CL_TOLEDO_VU' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CONCILIG%' THEN 'CONCILIG'
	
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%CRC%' THEN 'CRC'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%FLEX%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%LM%' THEN 'LM'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NOVA%' THEN 'LM'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%NO_ACTION_BV' THEN 'DIGITAL'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE 'PILOTO_BOM' THEN 'DIGITAL'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%PASCHOAL%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'
	
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%RN%' THEN 'PASCHOALLOTO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%SERVICES%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%TOLEDO%' THEN 'TOLEDO'
	
	WHEN ASSESSORIA LIKE '%VILELA%' THEN 'DESCADASTRADO'
	END AS ASSESSORIA_CORR,
	CHAVE AS CONTRATO,
	VR_SALDO_ATUAL AS VLR_COBRANCA
	FROM WORK.CARTEIRA_CL;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CART_UNICA_FULL_PGTO AS
SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN B.DT_REGULARIZADO IS NOT MISSING THEN VLR_COBRANCA ELSE 0 END AS RISCO_BAIXADO,
B.DT_REGULARIZADO,
C.DU AS DU_PGTO,
CASE
WHEN A.FAIXA_ATRASO IN ('FLUXO_CA','FLUXO_CL') THEN D.DU
ELSE 1 END AS DU_ENTRADA
FROM CART_UNICA_FULL A
LEFT JOIN BASE_PGTOS_FULL B
ON A.contrato = B.CONTRATO
AND A.SAFRA = B.SAFRA
and A.ASSESSORIA_CORR = B.ASSESSORIA_CORR
left join BASE_DU C
on B.DT_REGULARIZADO =C.data
left join BASE_DU D
on A.DT_ENTRADA =D.data
WHERE A.contrato IS NOT MISSING
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL AS
SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 75 AND 360 THEN 'CL1'
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO > 360 THEN 'CL2'
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO < 75 OR A.ATR_DT_ACORDO IS MISSING THEN 'SEM_DT_AC'
ELSE 'VALIDAR' END AS FASE_ACORDO
FROM BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL A
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL_2 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE ,
           T1.FASE_ACORDO,

		  DATEPART(t1.DATA_PAGTO) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DATA_PAGTO,
          
            (MEAN(t1.QTDE_PARCELAS_ACORDO)) FORMAT=BEST2. AS PLANO_MEDIO,

          
		    (CASE
            WHEN t1.APROVADOR IS NOT MISSING THEN 'EXCECAO' ELSE 'ALCADA' END ) AS TIPO_APROVACAO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CDACORDO)) AS QTD,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RECEBIDO_BV,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_ENVIADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SALDO_ENVIADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.DESCONTO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS DESCONTO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRPRINCIPALNEGOCIACAO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VRPRINCIPALNEGOCIACAO
      FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.DATA_PAGTO,
			   T1.FASE_ACORDO,
               (CALCULATED TIPO_APROVACAO)

;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL_2 AS
   SELECT
A.*,

B.DU AS DU_PGTO
FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL_2 A
      LEFT JOIN
	  WORK.BASE_DU B
	  ON A.DATA_PAGTO = B.DATA

;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE PERF_CA AS
SELECT
distinct
SAFRA,
FX_ATRASO_NEW,
DU_REGULARIZADO,
SUM(VLR_COBRANCA) AS RISCO,
SUM(VLR_REGULARIZADO) AS REGULARIZADO
FROM PERF_CA
GROUP BY
SAFRA,
FX_ATRASO_NEW,
DU_REGULARIZADO
;
QUIT;
proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA   ;
run;

proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW   ;
run;

proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW DU_REGULARIZADO;
run;


data PERF_CA_ACUM;
    set PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW;

    retain risco_acum regularizado_acum 0;

    if first.FX_ATRASO_NEW then do;
        risco_acum = 0;
        regularizado_acum = 0;
    end;

    risco_acum + RISCO;
    regularizado_acum + REGULARIZADO;
run;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.resultado_ca AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (t1.regularizado_acum/t1.risco_acum) AS perf
      FROM WORK.PERF_CA_ACUM t1
      WHERE t1.SAFRA >= '1Oct2024'd;
QUIT;

/* TASK: BASE_REGRA_90_PORC */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 013 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: BASE_REGRA_90_PORC */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-MX6jvfORSlRY7IdP/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);

Create table WORK.BASE_ORA_VAL_ENTRADA_2025 as select

input(cats(put(ano,z4.),put(mes,z2.)),z6.) as safra,
CONTRATO,
input(NU_ACORDO,z8.) as NU_ACORDO,
DT_PGTO_ENTRADA,
VL_PG_ENTRADA,
IOF,
flag_isento,
DT_PROX_PARC,
VL_PARC,
ASSESSORIA,
PERC_PARC,
SALDO_CONTRATO,
AHAUTCOLLID,
desc_princ,
flag_status_entrada,
PGTO_PARC_POST,
input(COD_ASSESSORIA,z4.) as COD_ASSESSORIA,
COND_ATUAL_AC

from connection to perm
(
SELECT
A.*,
cast(B.APAMTPAY as float64) AS PGTO_PARC_POST,
c.AHCOLLID AS COD_ASSESSORIA,
C.AHDSCQB AS COND_ATUAL_AC
FROM
(SELECT
extract (year from  DT_PGTO_ENTRADA) as ano,
extract (month from date(DT_PGTO_ENTRADA)) as mes,
cast(contrato as int64) AS CONTRATO,
APAHID AS NU_ACORDO,
DT_PGTO_ENTRADA,
VL_PG_ENTRADA,
cast(AHVLIOF as float64) AS IOF,
flag_isento,
DT_PROX_PARC,
cast(VL_PARC as float64) as VL_PARC,
AGFIRM AS ASSESSORIA,
PERC_PARC,
DMCURBAL AS SALDO_CONTRATO,
AHAUTCOLLID,
desc_princ,


CASE
WHEN VL_PG_ENTRADA  / VL_PARC  < 90 THEN 'menor 90'
WHEN VL_PG_ENTRADA / VL_PARC >= 90 THEN 'maior 90'
ELSE 'validar' END AS flag_status_entrada

FROM

(
SELECT
  a.APAHID,
  a.APDETID,
  a.APINDATE,
  a.APDUEDT AS DT_PGTO_ENTRADA,
  a.APAMT,
  A.APAMTIOF,
 cast(a.APAMTPAY as float64) AS VL_PG_ENTRADA,
  a.APFLAG,
  b.APDETID,
  b.APDUEDT AS DT_PROX_PARC,
   cast(b.APAMT as float64) AS VL_PARC,
   c.AHAUTCOLLID,
  c.AHTOTPMT,
  c.AHPRD,
  c.AHVLIOF,
  cast(c.AHACCT as int64) AS contrato,
  c.AHFLTPIOF AS flag_isento,
  cast(c.AHDESCPAMT as float64) AS desc_princ,
  
 
cast(e.DMCURBAL as float64) AS DMCURBAL,
  d.AGFIRM,
    (cast(a.APAMTPAY as float64) /cast(b.APAMT as float64)) AS perc_parc
  FROM
(SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APAMTIOF , APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT, APAMTPAY, APFLAG
FROM

(SELECT APAHID, APDETID, APAMTIOF , APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT, APAMTPAY, APFLAG FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet UNION ALL
 SELECT APAHID, APDETID, APAMTIOF , APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT, APAMTPAY, APFLAG FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet_esp)

WHERE
EXTRACT(month FROM APDUEDT) >= 1 and
EXTRACT(YEAR FROM APDUEDT) = 2025
AND cast(APDETID as int64) = 0 AND cast(APAMTPAY as float64) > 0
ORDER BY APAHID DESC) a
LEFT JOIN
   (SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT, APAMTPAY, APFLAG
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet
WHERE cast(APDETID as int64) = 1
ORDER BY APAHID DESC) b
ON a.APAHID = b.APAHID


LEFT JOIN (SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHFLTPIOF, AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT, AHFREQ, AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF, AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr
UNION ALL
SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHFLTPIOF,AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT, AHFREQ, AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF , AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr_esp) c
ON a.APAHID = c.AHID


LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agency d
ON c.AHCOLLID = d.AGAGENCY

LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.delqmst e
ON c.AHACCT = e.DMACCT

WHERE b.APDETID IS NOT NULL
AND cast(a.APAMTPAY as float64) / cast(b.APAMT as float64) < 90
ORDER BY a.APDUEDT)

) A

LEFT JOIN
(SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APAMTIOF , APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT, APAMTPAY, APFLAG
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet
WHERE

EXTRACT(month FROM date(APDUEDT)) >= 1 and
EXTRACT(YEAR FROM APDUEDT) = 2025 
AND cast(APDETID as int64) = 1
AND cast(APAMTPAY as float64) > 0
ORDER BY APAHID DESC) B
ON A.NU_ACORDO = B.APAHID

LEFT JOIN (SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT, AHFREQ, AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF, AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr
UNION ALL
SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT, AHFREQ, AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF , AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr_esp) C
ON A.NU_ACORDO = C.AHID
);
disconnect from perm;
quit;

    select cats("PERF_CL.", memname)
    into :lista_tabelas separated by ' '
    from dictionary.tables
    where libname = 'PERF_CL'
      and memname like "&prefix%"
      and memname ne "&excluir";

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RESULTADO_CL AS
SELECT
A.*,
INPUT(CATS(PUT(PRODUTO,Z2.),PUT(NUCONTA,Z10.)),Z12.) AS CONTRATO,
case
    when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL < 100 then "a.ate_100"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 100 and 300 then "b.100_300"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 300 and 500 then "c.300_500"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 500 and 1000 then "d.500_1k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 1000 and 3000 then "e.1k_3k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 3000 and 5000 then "f.3k_5k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 5000 and 10000 then "g.5k_10k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 10000 and 20000 then "h.10k_20k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 20000 and 40000 then "i.20k_40k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 40000 and 60000 then "j.40k_60k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL between 60000 and 90000 then "k.60k_90k"
	when a.VR_SALDO_ATUAL > 90000  then "l.Maior_90k"
	else 'validar'
	end as RANGE_SALDO
FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL A
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_ACORDOS_MIS AS
select DATA_BASE,
CD_ASSESSORIA,
CASE
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4035 THEN 'PASCHOALOTTO'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4033 THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4037 THEN 'LM_ANGELIS'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4036 THEN 'BELLINATI'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4040 THEN 'BELLINATI_VU'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4061 THEN 'CONCILIG'
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA =  4041 THEN 'TOLEDO_VU'
ELSE 'VALIDAR' END AS ASSESSORIA,
CASE
WHEN CD_ASSESSORIA = 1015 THEN 'DIGITAL'
ELSE 'ASSESSORIA' END AS ORIGEM_AC,
case
when APROVADOR is not null then "EXCECAO"
ELSE "ALCADA" END AS APROVACAO ,
NUCONTA,
PRODUTO ,
CDACORDO ,
PARCELA_PAGA
from WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_MIS


;
QUIT;

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: FLUXO_CL */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 015 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: FLUXO_CL */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-ZzckIitljmvT6D4k/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);

Create table WORK.BASE_FLUXO_CL as select * from connection to perm
(SELECT
extract(year from DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO) || extract(month from (date(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO))) AS SAFRA_FLUXO,
CD_ASSESSORIA,
AGFIRM AS ASSESSORIA,
DMQUE  AS CART,
extract(day from (date(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO)))  AS DIA_MARCACAO,
extract(day from (date(DT_ENVIO)))  AS DIA_ENVIO,
count(NR_CONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
SUM(cast(VL_DIVIDA as float64)) AS VL_DIVIDA,
CASE
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) = 75 THEN 'a.75'
ELSE 'b.Maior_75'
END AS FAIXA,

CASE
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) < 75  THEN 'A.MENOR 75'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  75 AND 90  THEN 'B.75 A 90'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  91 AND 120  THEN 'C.91 A 120'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  121 AND 150  THEN 'D.121 A 150'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  151 AND 180  THEN 'E.151 A 180'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  181 AND 210  THEN 'F.181 A 210'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  211 AND 240  THEN 'G.211 A 240'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  241 AND 270  THEN 'H.241 A 270'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  271 AND 300  THEN 'I.271 A 300'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  301 AND 330  THEN 'J.301 A 330'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) BETWEEN  331 AND 360  THEN 'J.331 A 360'
WHEN cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) >360  THEN 'K.MAIOR 360'
ELSE 'VALIDAR'
END FAIXA_ATRASO
FROM
( SELECT A. *
, B.AGFIRM
,C.u3cdleg
FROM (SELECT NR_CONTRATO AS NR_CONTRATO
                      
                      , NR_DIAS_ATRASO
                      , DMQUE
                      , CD_ASSESSORIA
                      , VL_DIVIDA
                      , DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO
                      , DATE_ADD(DATE(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO), INTERVAL 1 DAY ) AS DT_ENVIO
                  FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.tb_historico_disponibilizacao
                  WHERE
                  DMQUE IN ('CL') AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO)= 2025
                  AND CD_ASSESSORIA NOT IN ('1018','1020')
) A
LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agency B ON A.CD_ASSESSORIA = B.AGAGENCY
LEFT JOIN bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.uda3 C ON A.NR_CONTRATO = C.U3ACCT)

GROUP BY
NR_DIAS_ATRASO,
CD_ASSESSORIA,
AGFIRM,
DMQUE,
DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO,
DT_ENVIO
)
;
disconnect from perm;
quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: FLUXO_CL */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 015 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: 5MAIORES_SALDOS_CL */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 016 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: 5MAIORES_SALDOS_CL */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-9Cc5zxOWEHqQRJ49/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA BASE_MAIORES_SALDOS;
SET WORK.BASE_MAIOR_SALDO_ATIVOS;
where maior_saldo > 0 AND ASSESSORIA IS NOT NULL and ASSESSORIA <> '#N/A'

;
RUN;

proc sort data=BASE_MAIORES_SALDOS;
    by DATA_BASE ASSESSORIA fx_atraso descending MAIOR_SALDO;
run;

proc rank data=BASE_MAIORES_SALDOS out=ranked_saldos ties=low descending;
    by DATA_BASE ASSESSORIA fx_atraso;
    var MAIOR_SALDO ;
    ranks rnk;
run;

data top5_saldos;
    set ranked_saldos;
    if rnk <= 5;

run;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: 5MAIORES_SALDOS_CL */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 016 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* TASK: VALIDACAO ENTRADA */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 017 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: VALIDACAO ENTRADA */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-WTYjzQXeGC2iWq5q/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA B_VAL_ENTRADA_CL;
SET WORK.BASE_ORA_VAL_ENTRADA_202:;

RUN;

DATA B_CART_CL;
SET PERF_CL.CART_COB_DIST_CL_202:;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RISCO_ACUMULADO AS
SELECT
DATA_BASE,
TIPO,
COD_ASSESS,
FX_ATRASO,
SUM(SALDO_RISCO) AS RISCO_TOTAL
FROM WORK.B_CART_CL
GROUP BY
DATA_BASE,
TIPO,
COD_ASSESS,
FX_ATRASO
;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE B_VALIDA_ENTRADA_BI AS
SELECT
A.*,
B.FX_ATRASO,
B.TIPO,
C.RISCO_TOTAL
FROM WORK.B_VAL_ENTRADA_CL A
LEFT JOIN WORK.B_CART_CL B
ON A.SAFRA = B.DATA_BASE AND
A.COD_ASSESSORIA = B.COD_ASSESS AND
A.CONTRATO =B.CHAVE
LEFT JOIN WORK.RISCO_ACUMULADO C
ON A.SAFRA = C.DATA_BASE AND
A.COD_ASSESSORIA = C.COD_ASSESS AND
B.TIPO = C.TIPO AND
B.FX_ATRASO = C.FX_ATRASO

;
RUN;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: VALIDACAO ENTRADA */

%let DATA = '2025-07-01' ;

%let cd_assesoria = ('4035','4033','4061','4036','4024');
%let ANOMES = 202507;

;
DATA BASE_CARTEIRA_MIS;
SET WORK.RESULTADO_CL;
IF ASSESSORIA ='CONCILIG' THEN ASSESSORIA = 'CONCILIG';
IF ASSESSORIA ='BELLINATI' THEN ASSESSORIA = 'BELLINATI';
IF ASSESSORIA ='TOLEDO' THEN ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO';
IF ASSESSORIA ='RN ADV' THEN ASSESSORIA = 'PASCHOALOTTO';
keep CONTRATO DATA_BASE TIPO FX_ATRASO ASSESSORIA DATA_PAGTO STATUS vr_saldo_atual;
WHERE DATA_BASE = &ANOMES;
RUN;

Create table BASE_CARTEIRA as select * from connection to perm
(SELECT SAFE_CAST(cd_contrato AS INT64) as cd_contrato,
dt_criacao_base,
cd_assessoria,
nm_assessoria,

 FROM  bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.carteiragoe_cobranca_fato

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CARTEIRA AS
SELECT
A.*,
B.*
FROM BASE_CARTEIRA A
LEFT JOIN BASE_CARTEIRA_MIS B
ON A.cd_contrato = B.CONTRATO
WHERE B.CONTRATO IS NOT NULL;
;
QUIT;


proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);

Create table WORK.TAB_ACIONAMENTO_ANA as select * from connection to perm
( SELECT
    A.dt_acionamento AS DTREFERENCIA,
    SAFE_CAST(a.cd_contrato AS INT64) AS NUCONTRATO,
    A.nm_assessoria_bv,
    A.cd_assessoria,

	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ACIONAMENTO,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(0) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS TENTATIVA_S_RING,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) <> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS TENTATIVA_C_RING,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(1) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS SEM_ATENIMENTO,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(2,3,4,5,6) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS COM_ATENDIDAS,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(2) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATENDIMENTO_IMPROD,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(3,4,5,6) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATENDIMENTO_PROD,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(2,3) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_SEM_CPC_CPCA,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(4) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_CPC,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(5) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_CPCA,
    CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(4,5) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_CPC_CPCA,
    CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IN(6) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS PROMESSA,

	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_atendimento AS INT64) IN(0) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_HUMANO,
	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_atendimento AS INT64) IN(1) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS ATEND_DIGITAL,

	CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) <> 0  THEN COUNT(DISTINCT (a.cd_telefone_contato)) ELSE 0 END AS TELEFONES_DISTINTOS,


CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) = 5 THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS WHATSAPP,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) = 4 THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS BOLETAGEM,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) IN(1,2) THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS SMS,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) IN(9,10) THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS CHAT,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) IN(0) THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS DAC,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) IN(8) THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS AGENTE_VIRTUAL,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_canal_atendimento AS INT64) IN(3,6,7,11,12) THEN COUNT(A.tp_canal_atendimento) ELSE 0 END AS DEMAIS_CANAIS,

CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_discagem AS INT64) IN(1) THEN COUNT(A.tp_discagem) ELSE 0 END AS RECEPTIVO,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_discagem AS INT64) IN(0) THEN COUNT(A.tp_discagem) ELSE 0 END AS ATIVO,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_discagem AS INT64) IN(2) THEN COUNT(A.tp_discagem) ELSE 0 END AS ATIVO_MANUAL,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_discagem AS INT64) IN(3) THEN COUNT(A.tp_discagem) ELSE 0 END AS SEM_DISCAGEM,
CASE WHEN SAFE_CAST(A.tp_ocorrencia_chamada AS INT64) IN(13) THEN COUNT(A.tp_ocorrencia_chamada) ELSE 0 END AS REDISCAGEM

FROM
    `bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.assessoria_acionamentos_cobranca_fato` AS A
WHERE
    A.dt_acionamento BETWEEN &DATA AND &DATA_FIM
    AND SAFE_CAST(A.tp_resultado_acionamento AS INT64) IS NOT NULL

    AND cd_assessoria  in &cd_assesoria
    AND A.cd_contrato IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY
    A.tp_ocorrencia_chamada,
    A.tp_discagem,
    A.tp_canal_atendimento,
    A.tp_atendimento,
    A.tp_resultado_acionamento,
    A.dt_acionamento,
    A.cd_contrato,
    A.cd_assessoria,
    A.nm_assessoria_bv);
disconnect from perm;
quit;


PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE TAB_ACIONAMENTO AS
SELECT
DTREFERENCIA,
NUCONTRATO,
nm_assessoria_bv,
cd_assessoria,
CASE WHEN SUM(ACIONAMENTO) >= 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ACIONAMENTO,
CASE WHEN SUM(TENTATIVA_S_RING) >= 1   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  TENTATIVA_S_RING,
CASE WHEN SUM(TENTATIVA_C_RING) >= 1   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  TENTATIVA_C_RING,
CASE WHEN SUM(SEM_ATENIMENTO) >= 1   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  SEM_ATENIMENTO,
CASE WHEN SUM(COM_ATENDIDAS) >= 1   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  COM_ATENDIDAS,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATENDIMENTO_IMPROD) >= 1   THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS   ATENDIMENTO_IMPROD,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATENDIMENTO_PROD) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATENDIMENTO_PROD,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATEND_SEM_CPC_CPCA) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATEND_SEM_CPC_CPCA,


CASE WHEN SUM(ATEND_CPC_CPCA) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATEND_CPC_CPCA,
CASE WHEN SUM(PROMESSA) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  PROMESSA,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATEND_HUMANO) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATEND_HUMANO,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATEND_DIGITAL) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATEND_DIGITAL,

round(TELEFONES_DISTINTOS,0.01) AS  TELEFONES_DISTINTOS,
CASE WHEN SUM(WHATSAPP)  >= 1    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  WHATSAPP,
CASE WHEN SUM(BOLETAGEM)  >= 1    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  BOLETAGEM,
CASE WHEN SUM(SMS)  >= 1    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  SMS,
CASE WHEN SUM(CHAT) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  CHAT,
CASE WHEN SUM(DAC) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  DAC,
CASE WHEN SUM(AGENTE_VIRTUAL) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  AGENTE_VIRTUAL,
CASE WHEN SUM(DEMAIS_CANAIS) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  DEMAIS_CANAIS,
CASE WHEN SUM(RECEPTIVO) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  RECEPTIVO,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATIVO) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATIVO,
CASE WHEN SUM(ATIVO_MANUAL) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS  ATIVO_MANUAL,
CASE WHEN SUM(SEM_DISCAGEM) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS SEM_DISCAGEM,
CASE WHEN SUM(REDISCAGEM) >= 1     THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS REDISCAGEM
FROM WORK.TAB_ACIONAMENTO_ANA

GROUP BY
DTREFERENCIA,
NUCONTRATO,
nm_assessoria_bv,
cd_assessoria
;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CONSOLIDADA AS
SELECT
A.dt_criacao_base,
C.DU,
c.DsDiaSemana,
A.cd_contrato,
A.cd_assessoria,
A.nm_assessoria,
D.ASSESSORIA_NORM,
A.vr_risco_contrato,
A.ds_produto_contrato,
a.TIPO,
a.FX_ATRASO,
case when a.DATA_PAGTO <> . then "pg" else "n_pg" end as status_pgto,
a.STATUS,
a.vr_saldo_atual,
B.*
FROM WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA A
LEFT JOIN WORK.TAB_ACIONAMENTO B
ON A.cd_contrato = B.NUCONTRATO AND A.dt_criacao_base = B.DTREFERENCIA

LEFT JOIN B_GERAL.DIAS_UTEIS C
ON A.dt_criacao_base = C.DATA
LEFT JOIN WORK.TAB_ASSESSORIAS_NORMALIZADAS D

ON A.cd_assessoria = D.CD_ASSESSORIA

;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACIONA_DIA_CL AS
   SELECT t1.dt_criacao_base AS DATA_REF,
          t1.ASSESSORIA_NORM,
          t1.Du,
          t1.DsDiaSemana,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.status_pgto,
          
            (SUM(t1.vr_risco_contrato)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RISCO_CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RISCO_CARTEIRA_MIS,
          
            (COUNT(t1.cd_contrato)) AS CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.ACIONAMENTO)) AS ACIONAMENTOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.TENTATIVA_S_RING)) AS TENTATIVA_S_RING,
          
            (SUM(t1.TENTATIVA_C_RING)) AS TENTATIVA_C_RING,
          
            (SUM(t1.SEM_ATENIMENTO)) AS SEM_ATENIMENTO,
          
            (SUM(t1.COM_ATENDIDAS)) AS COM_ATENDIMENTO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATENDIMENTO_IMPROD)) AS ATENDIMENTO_IMPROD,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATENDIMENTO_PROD)) AS ATENDIMENTO_PROD,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATEND_SEM_CPC_CPCA)) AS ATEND_SEM_CPC_CPCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATEND_CPC_CPCA)) AS ATEND_CPC_CPCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.PROMESSA)) AS PROMESSA,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATEND_HUMANO)) AS ATEND_HUMANO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATEND_DIGITAL)) AS ATEND_DIGITAL,
          
            (MEAN(t1.TELEFONES_DISTINTOS)) AS MEDIA_TEL_DISTINTOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.WHATSAPP)) AS WHATSAPP,
          
            (SUM(t1.BOLETAGEM)) AS BOLETAGEM,
          
            (SUM(t1.SMS)) AS SMS,
          
            (SUM(t1.CHAT)) AS CHAT,
          
            (SUM(t1.DAC)) AS DAC,
          
            (SUM(t1.AGENTE_VIRTUAL)) AS AGENTE_VIRTUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.DEMAIS_CANAIS)) AS DEMAIS_CANAIS,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEPTIVO)) AS RECEPTIVO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATIVO)) AS ATIVO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATIVO_MANUAL)) AS ATIVO_MANUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.SEM_DISCAGEM)) AS SEM_DISCAGEM,
          
            (SUM(t1.REDISCAGEM)) AS REDISCAGEM
      FROM WORK.BASE_CONSOLIDADA t1
      WHERE t1.DsDiaSemana NOT IN ('sábado','domingo')
      GROUP BY t1.dt_criacao_base,
               t1.ASSESSORIA_NORM,
               t1.Du,
               t1.DsDiaSemana,
               t1.TIPO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.status_pgto;
QUIT;

proc sql;

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 021 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-p2tB7tjjrzfrZdPa/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%LET SAFRA = 2025;

proc sql;
	connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
		credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
		schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage MAX_CHAR_LEN=100 );
	Create table BASE_IOF_2025 as select * from connection to perm
		(

SELECT
SAFRA,
cod_assessoria,
nome_assessoria,
tipo_acordo,
status_iof,
status_pgto,
SUM(qtd) AS qtd,
SUM(vlr_acordo) AS vlr_acordo,
SUM(iof_devido) AS iof_devido,
FLAG_EST_IOF
from
(

SELECT
cast(extract( year from AHDT) || LPAD(CAST(EXTRACT(MONTH FROM AHDT) AS STRING), 2, '0') as int64) AS SAFRA,
CAST(AHCOLLID AS INT64) AS cod_assessoria,
AGFIRM AS nome_assessoria,
CASE WHEN CAST(AHPRD AS INT64) >= 1 THEN 'parcelado' ELSE 'a_vista' END AS tipo_acordo,
AHFLTPIOF AS status_iof,
CASE WHEN CAST(APAMTPAY AS FLOAT64) > 0 THEN 'pago' ELSE 'n_pago' END AS status_pgto,
COUNT(AHACCT) AS qtd,
SUM(cast(AHTOTPMT as float64)) AS vlr_acordo,
SUM(cast(AHVLIOF as float64)) AS iof_devido,
FLAG_EST_IOF

FROM
(
SELECT
A.AHID,
A.AHDT,
A.AHACCT,
A.AHVLIOF,
A.AHTOTPMT,

A.AHPRD,
A.AHCOLLID,
A.AHFLTPIOF,
c.AGFIRM,
b.APAMT,
b.APAMTPAY,

CASE WHEN cast(d.U3SCORE4 as int64) = 3535 THEN 'S' ELSE 'N' END AS FLAG_EST_IOF

FROM
(SELECT * FROM
(SELECT
AHID,AHDT,AHACCT, AHVLIOF,AHTOTPMT,AHPRD ,AHCOLLID,AHFLTPIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr`
UNION ALL
SELECT
AHID,AHDT,AHACCT,AHVLIOF,AHTOTPMT,AHPRD,AHCOLLID,AHFLTPIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr_esp`)
WHERE extract( year from AHDT) =2025

) a

LEFT JOIN
(SELECT * FROM
(SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT,APAMT, APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet`
UNION all
SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT,APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet_esp`) WHERE cast(APDETID as int64)= 0 ) b
ON a.AHID = b.APAHID
LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agency` C
ON A.AHCOLLID =C.AGAGENCY

LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.uda3` d
ON A.AHACCT = d.U3ACCT

)

GROUP BY

AHDT,
AHCOLLID,
AGFIRM ,
AHPRD,
AHFLTPIOF,
APAMTPAY,
FLAG_EST_IOF
)

GROUP BY

SAFRA,
cod_assessoria,
nome_assessoria,
tipo_acordo,
status_iof,
status_pgto,
FLAG_EST_IOF

);
disconnect from perm;
quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 021 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: CONSOLIDANDO DADOS */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 022 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 579 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: CONSOLIDANDO DADOS */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-neTwUPLegN6QVF3d/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA BASE_IOF_CONSOL;
SET WORK.BASE_IOF_20:;
RUN;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_IOF_CONSOL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_IOF_CONSOL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA,
          t2.ASSESSORIA_PERF,
          t1.TIPO_ACORDO,
          t1.STATUS_IOF,
          t1.STATUS_PGTO,
          t1.QTD,
          t1.VLR_ACORDO,
          t1.IOF_DEVIDO,
		  t1.FLAG_EST_IOF,
		  case when t1.IOF_DEVIDO = 0 then 'SEM IOF' ELSE 'COM IOF' END AS STATUS_VLR_IOF
      FROM WORK.BASE_IOF_CONSOL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_ASSEESORIAS_CRUZAMENTO t2 ON (t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = t2.COD_ASSESSORIA);

/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: LOCALIZAR RESULTS CRM GOE */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-n2w7AtgPEYpKVW99/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=1000);

Create table WORK.TAB_MUDOS_E_DESL as select * from connection to perm
( SELECT

nm_assessoria,

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: LOCALIZAR RESULTS CRM GOE */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 023 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: CORREÇÃO JOAO */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 024 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 654 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: CORREÇÃO JOAO */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-9xeAHNX75kPznc4f/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME CARTAO  BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/erocha/CARTAO/MOVTO';
libname CARD_ACR BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/ranapole/Cartoes/Relatorios_Acordos/Entrada/';
LIBNAME B_GERAL     BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/malmeida';
LIBNAME CARNEIRO BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/jcarneiro';
LIBNAME ACris BASE '/riscosbv/cobranca/cristiano';

%LET SAFRA = '01jan2024'D;

DATA PERF_AMIGAVEL;
SET CARTAO.PERF_AMIGAVEL;
WHERE SAFRA >= &SAFRA ;

CDDIMCONTACARTAO = 1000000000*PRODUTO+NUCONTA;
IF TPCLASSE = 'F' THEN DT_ATRASO = DATEPART(DTENVIO_CYBER)-DIAS_ATRASO;
ELSE DT_ATRASO = DT_INICIO_ATRASO;
FORMAT DT_ATRASO DDMMYY10.;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE PERF_AMIGAVEL_B AS SELECT
A.*, B.VLR_COB_INI FROM PERF_AMIGAVEL A LEFT JOIN
CARNEIRO.CARTEIRA_CA B
ON A.NUCONTA = B.NUCONTA AND A.PRODUTO = B.PRODUTO AND A.DT_ATRASO = B.DT_INICIO_ATRASO;
QUIT;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE PERF_AUX_1 AS SELECT
A.*, B.ContadorFluxo, C.ContadorReg,
	CASE
		WHEN A.DT_ARQUIVO = C.ArquivoMax AND A.DTENVIO = C.EnvioMax THEN A.DT_REGULARIZADO
		ELSE .
	END AS DT_REGULARIZACAO FORMAT DDMMYY10.,
	CASE
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 0 THEN "FLUXO"
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 0 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 16 THEN "0001 A 0015"
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 16 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 31 THEN "0016 A 0030"
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 31 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 46 THEN "0031 A 0045"
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 46 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 61 THEN "0046 A 0060"
		WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 60 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 76 THEN "0061 A 0075"
         
        WHEN A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO > 75 AND A.SAFRA - A.DT_ATRASO <= 95 THEN "0076 A 0095"
	END AS FX_ATRASO_REAL
FROM PERF_AMIGAVEL_B A LEFT JOIN
(SELECT CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO, COUNT(*) AS ContadorFluxo
FROM PERF_AMIGAVEL_B WHERE TPCLASSE = "F" GROUP BY CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO) B
ON A.CDDIMCONTACARTAO = B.CDDIMCONTACARTAO AND A.DT_ATRASO = B.DT_ATRASO
LEFT JOIN
(SELECT CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO, COUNT(*) AS ContadorReg,
MAX(DT_ARQUIVO) AS ArquivoMax FORMAT DDMMYY10., MAX(DTENVIO) AS EnvioMax FORMAT DDMMYY10.
FROM PERF_AMIGAVEL_B WHERE DT_REGULARIZADO <> . GROUP BY CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO) C
ON A.CDDIMCONTACARTAO = C.CDDIMCONTACARTAO AND A.DT_ATRASO = C.DT_ATRASO
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL AS SELECT
A.*,
C.ContadorReg2
FROM PERF_AUX_1 A LEFT JOIN
(SELECT CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO, COUNT(*) AS ContadorReg2
FROM PERF_AUX_1 WHERE DT_REGULARIZACAO <> . GROUP BY CDDIMCONTACARTAO, DT_ATRASO) C
ON A.CDDIMCONTACARTAO = C.CDDIMCONTACARTAO AND A.DT_ATRASO = C.DT_ATRASO
ORDER BY SAFRA
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL_2 AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DT_ARQUIVO,
          t1.DTENVIO_CYBER,
          t1.DTENVIO,
          t1.DU_ENVIO,
          t1.TPCLASSE,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.NUCONTA,

            (CASE
            WHEN t1.SAFRA BETWEEN '01MAR2024'D AND '30JUN2024'D THEN t1.DT_REGULARIZACAO ELSE t1.DT_REGULARIZADO END)
            FORMAT=CATDFDD10. AS DT_REG_FINAL

/* TASK: ENTRATES_CA_GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 026 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 654 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: ENTRATES_CA_GCP */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-HWesnHkDdQYmaaFz/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);

Create table WORK.BASE_2025_ENTRANTES_CA as select
input(cats(ano,put(mes,z2.)),z6.) as SAFRA_FLUXO,
*
from connection to perm
(

SELECT
cast((EXTRACT(YEAR FROM date(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO))||EXTRACT(month FROM date(cast(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO as string)))) as int64) AS SAFRA_FLUXO,
cast(EXTRACT(day FROM DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO) as int64) AS dia,
cast(EXTRACT(month FROM date(cast(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO as string))) as int64) AS mes,
cast(EXTRACT(year FROM DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO) as int64) AS ano,
date(DT_ENVIO) AS dt_receb_ass,
cast(CD_ASSESSORIA as int64) as CD_ASSESSORIA,
cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) AS ATRASO_ENVIO,
cast(NR_DIAS_ATRASO as int64) + 1 AS ATRASO_RECEBIDO_ASS,
AGFIRM AS ASSESSORIA,
DMQUE  AS CART,
cast(EXTRACT(day FROM DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO) as int64) AS DIA_ENVIO,
cast(EXTRACT(day FROM DT_ENVIO) as int64) AS DIA_RECEBIDO_ASS,
cast(COUNT(NR_CONTRATO) as int64) AS qtd,
SUM(cast(VL_DIVIDA as float64)) AS VL_DIVIDA

FROM
(
 SELECT A. *
                 , B.AGFIRM
                 ,C.u3cdleg
                 , D.dmstatus
                 , D.DMAMTDLQ
                 , D.DMCURBAL
            FROM (SELECT NR_CONTRATO AS NR_CONTRATO

                      , NR_DIAS_ATRASO
                      , VL_DIVIDA
                      , DMQUE
                      , CD_ASSESSORIA
                      , DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO
                      , DATE_ADD(DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO, INTERVAL 1 DAY) AS DT_ENVIO

                  FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.tb_historico_disponibilizacao`
                  WHERE
                  DMQUE IN ('CA') AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO) =2025
                  AND CD_ASSESSORIA NOT in ('1018','1020','4010')
) A
                LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agency` B ON A.CD_ASSESSORIA = B.AGAGENCY
                LEFT JOIN  `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.uda3` C ON A.NR_CONTRATO = C.U3ACCT
                LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.delqmst` D ON A.NR_CONTRATO = D.DMACCT)
                WHERE  u3cdleg <> '2' 
                AND dmstatus IS NULL
GROUP BY
NR_DIAS_ATRASO,
CD_ASSESSORIA,
AGFIRM,
DMQUE,
DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO,
DT_ENVIO,
dmstatus

;



);
disconnect from perm;
quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: RELACAO_REMUN_PA */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 036 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 754 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: RELACAO_REMUN_PA */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-jZhiTVR6mecmhfv1/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.CAPACITY_CONTAS_OP_2);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.CAPACITY_CONTAS_OP_2 AS
   SELECT 
            (input(cats(put(year(t1.'Mês'n),z4.),put(month(t1.'Mês'n),z2.)),z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          t1.'Mês'n,
          

		  CASE
WHEN UPCASE(t1.Assessoria) IN('BELLINATI') THEN 4006
WHEN UPCASE(t1.Assessoria) IN('TOLEDO') THEN 4001
WHEN UPCASE(t1.Assessoria) IN('CONCILIG') THEN 4062
WHEN UPCASE(t1.Assessoria) IN('CRC') THEN 4007
WHEN UPCASE(t1.Assessoria) IN('PASCHOALOTTO','RN_ADV') THEN 4005
ELSE 0
END AS COD_ASSESSORIA,
            (UPCASE(t1.Assessoria)) AS ASSESSORIA,
          t1.Carteira,
          
            (ROUND(t1.TOTAL_M_DIA)) AS LOGADOS_M_DIA,
          
            (ROUND((t1.TOTAL_M_DIA / 2))) AS PAS_M_DIA
      FROM WORK.CAPACITY_CONTAS_OP t1
      WHERE t1.Carteira IN
           (
           'CARTAO_AMIGAVEL'
           );
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.REMUENRACAO_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.REMUENRACAO_CA AS
   SELECT
(input(cats(put(year(t1.PERIODO_PGTO),z4.),put(month(t1.PERIODO_PGTO),z2.)),z6.)) AS SAFRA_PGTO,

          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('BELLINATI','BELLINATI_2') THEN 'BELLINATI'
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('TOLEDO','TOLEDO_2') THEN 'TOLEDO'
            ELSE t1.Assessoria
            END) AS ASSESSORIA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_PAGAR)) FORMAT=BEST12. AS VALOR_PAGAR,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('BELLINATI','BELLINATI_2') THEN 4006
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('TOLEDO','TOLEDO_2') THEN 4001
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('CONCILIG') THEN 4062
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('CRC') THEN 4007
            WHEN t1.Assessoria IN('PASCHOALOTTO') THEN 4005
            ELSE 0
            END) AS COD_ASSESSORIA
      FROM WORK.CONSOLIDADO_CA t1
      GROUP BY t1.PERIODO_PGTO,
               (CALCULATED ASSESSORIA),
               (CALCULATED COD_ASSESSORIA);
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_REMUENRACAO_CA_BI);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_REMUENRACAO_CA_BI AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.'Mês'n,
          t1.COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.Carteira,
          t1.LOGADOS_M_DIA,
          t1.PAS_M_DIA,
          t2.VALOR_PAGAR FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VALOR_PAGAR,
          
            (t2.VALOR_PAGAR / t1.PAS_M_DIA) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS REMUNERACAO_PA
      FROM WORK.CAPACITY_CONTAS_OP_2 t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.REMUENRACAO_CA t2 ON (t1.SAFRA = t2.SAFRA_PGTO) AND (t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = t2.COD_ASSESSORIA);
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: RELACAO_REMUN_PA */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 036 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: VALIDACAO EVOLUCAO < 50 REAIS */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 037 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 760 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: VALIDACAO EVOLUCAO < 50 REAIS */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-w9hEyWHlBfjnEpYh/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA BASE_RESULTADOS;
SET WORK.RESULTADO_CL;
IF RECEBIDO <> . AND RECEBIDO < 50 THEN FLAG_ENTRADA = 'MENOR_50_REAIS';
ELSE IF RECEBIDO <> . AND RECEBIDO = 50 THEN FLAG_ENTRADA = '50 REAIS';
ELSE IF RECEBIDO <> . AND RECEBIDO > 50 THEN FLAG_ENTRADA = 'MAIOR_50 REAIS';

IF ORIGEM_AC IS NULL AND RECEBIDO_PERF <> . THEN ORIGEM = 'ASSESSORIA'
ELSE IF  ORIGEM_AC IS NOT NULL AND RECEBIDO_PERF = . THEN ORIGEM =''
ELSE t2.ORIGEM_AC
END
;
RUN;

DATA HIST_CASOS_MENOR_50;

DATA BASE_REM_CL;
SET
WORK.'2024.07_CL'N
WORK.'2024.08_CL'N

RUN;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_REM_CL AS
SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 61 AND 90 THEN "A.61 A 90"
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 91 AND 120 THEN "B.91 A 120"
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 121 AND 180 THEN "C.121 A 180"
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 181 AND 360 THEN "D.181 A 360"
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO BETWEEN 361 AND 1080 THEN "E.361 A 1080"
WHEN A.ATR_DT_ACORDO > 1080 THEN "F.ACIMA 1080"
ELSE 'G.ABAIXO 60'
END AS FAIXA_ATR_AC

FROM BASE_REM_CL A
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_REM_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.CL,
          t1.FAIXA_ATR_AC,
          (COUNT(t1.CDACORDO)) AS QTD_AC,
          (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS TOTAL_RECEBIDO_BV,
          (SUM(t1.remuneracao)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS TOTAL_REMUNERACAO
      FROM WORK.BASE_REM_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.CL,
               t1.FAIXA_ATR_AC;
QUIT;

/* TASK: BASES_CONSOLIDADAS */

/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 844 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_PERF_ATUAL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DIAS_ATRASO,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.VLR_COBRANCA,
		  t1.DT_REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.BASE_PERF t1
      WHERE t1.ASSESSORIA NOT = 'PILOTO_BOM' AND t1.SAFRA = '1Jan2025'd
      ORDER BY t1.VLR_COBRANCA DESC;
QUIT;

DATA BASE_ATRASO_ANT;
SET
WORK.BASE_ULTI_ATRASO_202:;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ATRASO_ANT_FINAL AS
   SELECT

          t1.NuContrato,
          t1.TpProduto,
		  MIN(t1.NuDiasAtraso) AS MENOR_ATRASO,
          ROUND(MEAN(t1.NuDiasAtraso)) AS ATRASO_MEDIO,
		  MAX(t1.NuDiasAtraso) AS MAIOR_ATRASO
          

      FROM WORK.BASE_ATRASO_ANT t1
GROUP BY

          t1.NuContrato,
          t1.TpProduto ;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_PERF_ATUAL_ENR AS
   SELECT
   A.*,
   B.*
      FROM BASE_PERF_ATUAL A
LEFT JOIN BASE_ATRASO_ANT_FINAL B
ON A.CHAVE = B.NuContrato
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BASILEIA_ATUAL AS
		SELECT
			(INPUT(CATS(PUT(PRODUTO,Z2.),PUT(NUCONTA,Z10.)),Z12.)) AS CONTRATO,
			DATA_ARQ,
			NUCONTA,
			PRODUTO,
			DIAS_AT,
			VALOR
		FROM BASILEIAS_MELIUS WHERE DATA_ARQ = (SELECT MAX(DATA_ARQ) FROM BASILEIAS_MELIUS);
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_MELIUS_ENRRIQUECIDA AS
SELECT
A.*,
B.DIAS_AT AS ATRASO_BL
FROM BASE_MELIUS A
LEFT JOIN BASILEIA_ATUAL B
ON A.DMACCT = B.CONTRATO
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASILEIA_ATUAL AS
   SELECT t1.CONTRATO,
          (LENGTH(PUT(t1.CONTRATO,ZN.))) AS Calculation
      FROM WORK.BASILEIA_ATUAL t1
WHERE (LENGTH(PUT(t1.CONTRATO,ZN.))) > 13;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CP_GOE as
select
a.*,
input(cats(put(year(DT_CRIACAO_BASe_2),z4.),put(month(DT_CRIACAO_BASe_2),z2.)),z6.) as safra,
b.du,
b.DsDiaSemana
from
(select
a.*,
INPUT(a.DT_CRIACAO_BASE,anydtdte21.) format date9. AS DT_CRIACAO_BASe_2
from BASE_CP_GOE a) a
left join BASE_DU b
on a.DT_CRIACAO_BASe_2 = b.data
;
quit;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE VALIDA_CRC_AGO25 AS
SELECT
A.*,
B.DT_REGULARIZADO AS PGTO_AGO,
B.FX_ATRASO_NEW AS  FAIXA_AGO,
C.DT_REGULARIZADO AS PGTO_SET,
C.FX_ATRASO_NEW AS FAIXA_SET,
CASE
WHEN A.Data_Pgto <= A.Mailing_Min THEN 'ENTRADA_PAGA' ELSE '' END AS STATUS_ENTRADA
FROM BASE_VALIDACAO_CRC A
LEFT JOIN BASE_RESU_CA_AGO25 B
ON A.CONTRATO = B.CHAVE
LEFT JOIN BASE_RESU_CA_SET25 C
ON A.CONTRATO = C.CHAVE
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE VALIDA_CRC_AGO25_2 AS
SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN A.PGTO_AGO IS MISSING AND A.PGTO_SET IS MISSING AND A.FAIXA_SET IS MISSING AND A.STATUS_ENTRADA IS MISSING
THEN 'VALIDAR'
ELSE 'OK'
END AS STATUS_FINAL

FROM VALIDA_CRC_AGO25 A
;
QUIT;

/* TASK: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 070 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 983 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-AjKeleQH7Pv1gmoD/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%LET SAFRA = 2025;

proc sql;
	connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
		credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
		schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage MAX_CHAR_LEN=100 );
	Create table BASE_IOF_2025 as select * from connection to perm
		(
	SELECT
		safra,
		cod_assessoria,
		nome_assessoria,
		tipo_acordo,
		status_iof,
		status_pgto,
		SUM(qtd) AS qtd,
		SUM(vlr_acordo) AS vlr_acordo,
		SUM(iof_devido) AS iof_devido,
		FLAG_EST_IOF
	from
		(
	SELECT
	 cast(FORMAT_DATE('%Y%m', AHDT) as int64) as  SAFRA,
		CAST(AHCOLLID AS INT64) AS cod_assessoria,
		AGFIRM AS nome_assessoria,
	CASE
		WHEN CAST(AHPRD AS INT64) >= 1 THEN 'parcelado'
		ELSE 'a_vista'
	END
AS tipo_acordo,
	AHFLTPIOF AS status_iof,
CASE
	WHEN CAST(APAMTPAY AS FLOAT64) > 0 THEN 'pago'
	ELSE 'n_pago'
END
AS status_pgto,
COUNT(AHACCT) AS qtd,
SUM(cast(AHTOTPMT as float64)) AS vlr_acordo,
SUM(cast(AHVLIOF as float64)) AS iof_devido,
FLAG_EST_IOF
FROM
(
SELECT
A.AHID,
A.AHDT,
A.AHACCT,
A.AHVLIOF,
A.AHTOTPMT,

A.AHPRD,
A.AHCOLLID,
A.AHFLTPIOF,
c.AGFIRM,
b.APAMT,
b.APAMTPAY,

CASE
WHEN cast(d.U3SCORE4 as int64) = 3535 THEN 'S'
ELSE 'N'
END
AS FLAG_EST_IOF
FROM
(SELECT * FROM
(SELECT
AHID,AHDT,AHACCT, AHVLIOF,AHTOTPMT,AHPRD ,AHCOLLID,AHFLTPIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr`
UNION ALL
SELECT
AHID,AHDT,AHACCT,AHVLIOF,AHTOTPMT,AHPRD,AHCOLLID,AHFLTPIOF
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr_esp`)
WHERE extract( year from AHDT) =&safra.
	
		) a
	LEFT JOIN
		(SELECT * FROM
		(SELECT
			APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT,APAMT, APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
		FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet`
			UNION all
		SELECT
			APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT,APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
		FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet_esp`) WHERE cast(APDETID as int64)= 0 ) b
			ON a.AHID = b.APAHID
		LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agency` C
			ON A.AHCOLLID =C.AGAGENCY
		LEFT JOIN `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.uda3` d
			ON A.AHACCT = d.U3ACCT
		)
	GROUP BY
		AHDT,
		AHCOLLID,
		AGFIRM ,
		AHPRD,
		AHFLTPIOF,
		APAMTPAY,
		FLAG_EST_IOF
		)
	GROUP BY
		safra,
		cod_assessoria,
		nome_assessoria,
		tipo_acordo,
		status_iof,
		status_pgto,
		FLAG_EST_IOF
		;
		)
	;
	disconnect from perm;
quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: IOF_2025_GCP */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 070 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_IOF_CONSOL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA,
          t2.ASSESSORIA_PERF,
          t1.TIPO_ACORDO,
          t1.STATUS_IOF,
          t1.STATUS_PGTO,
          t1.QTD,
          t1.VLR_ACORDO,
          t1.IOF_DEVIDO,
		  t1.FLAG_EST_IOF,
		  case when t1.IOF_DEVIDO = 0 then 'SEM IOF' ELSE 'COM IOF' END AS STATUS_VLR_IOF
      FROM WORK.BASE_IOF_CONSOL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_ASSEESORIAS_CRUZAMENTO t2 ON (t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = t2.COD_ASSESSORIA);
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_LM_0000 AS
   SELECT 
            ('LM_CL') AS CART_ORIGEM,
          t1.STATUS,
          t1.CdAssessoria,
          t1.NmAssessoria,
          t1.TpProduto,
          t1.STATUS_QUITACAO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NuContratoGestao)) AS COUNT_of_NuContratoGestao,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO_REDIST)) AS COUNT_of_CONTRATO_REDIST
      FROM WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_LM t1
      GROUP BY (CALCULATED CART_ORIGEM),
               t1.STATUS,
               t1.CdAssessoria,
               t1.NmAssessoria,
               t1.TpProduto,
               t1.STATUS_QUITACAO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_LM AS
   SELECT t1.NuContratoGestao,
          t1.STATUS,
          t2.CdAssessoria,
          t2.NmAssessoria,
          t2.TpProduto,
          t3.STATUS_QUITACAO,
          t4.DMACCT AS CONTRATO_REDIST
      FROM WORK.BASE_LM t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.CART_CONSO_CYBER_CARTOES t2 ON (t1.NuContratoGestao = t2.NuContrato)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.CART_COLCHAO_QUITADO t3 ON (t1.NuContratoGestao = t3.DMACCT)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_REDIST_31102024 t4 ON (t1.NuContratoGestao = t4.DMACCT);
QUIT;

/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */

/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_CA_BI);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_CA_BI AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DU_ENVIO_CART,
          t1.DIA_ENVIO,
          t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN T1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = 'FLUXO' THEN 'a.FLUXO'
            WHEN T1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = 'ATE 30 DIAS DE ATRASO' THEN 'b.ATE 30 DIAS DE ATRASO'
            WHEN T1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = 'DE 31 A 60 DIAS DE ATRASO' THEN 'c.DE 31 A 60 DIAS DE ATRASO'
            WHEN T1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = 'ACIMA DE 60 DIAS DE ATRASO' THEN 'd.ACIMA DE 60 DIAS DE ATRASO'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.FAIXA_QUEBRADA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.TPCLASSE,
          t1.RANGE_SALDO,
          t1.COUNT_of_CHAVE AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
          t1.SUM_of_VLR_COBRANCA AS VLR_COBRANCA,
          t1.SUM_of_VLR_REGULARIZADO AS VLR_REGULARIZADO,
          t1.FLAG_PILOTO_DIG,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.ASSESSORIA IN('CRC','CONCILIG') THEN 'PILOTO' ELSE 'TRADICIONAL' END) AS MODELO
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_CA t1;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 086 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_RESULTADO_CL_BI);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_RESULTADO_CL_BI AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DATA_BASE < 202510 and t1.FX_ATRASO ='75 A 120 ' THEN '1.75 A 120'
            WHEN t1.DATA_BASE >= 202510 and t1.FX_ATRASO ='75 A 120 ' THEN '2.95 A 120'
            WHEN  t1.FX_ATRASO ='95 A 120 ' THEN '2.95 A 120'
            WHEN t1.FX_ATRASO ='121 A 180 ' THEN '3.121 A 180'
            WHEN t1.FX_ATRASO ='181 A 360 ' THEN '4.181 A 360'
            WHEN t1.FX_ATRASO ='361 A 720 ' THEN '5.361 A 720'
            WHEN t1.FX_ATRASO ='ACIMA DE 720 ' THEN '6.ACIMA DE 720'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR' END) AS FX_ATRASO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.CL_STATUS = 1 THEN 'CL1'
            WHEN t1.CL_STATUS = 2 THEN 'CL2'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS CL_STATUS,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.RECEBIDO <> . AND t1.RECEBIDO < 50 THEN 'MENOR_50_REAIS'
            WHEN t1.RECEBIDO <> . AND t1.RECEBIDO = 50 THEN '50 REAIS'
            WHEN t1.RECEBIDO <> . AND t1.RECEBIDO > 50 THEN 'MAIOR_50 REAIS'
            ELSE ''
            END) AS FLAG_ENTRADA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.STATUS,
          t2.ORIGEM_AC,
          t2.APROVACAO,
          t1.RANGE_SALDO,
          t3.Du AS DU_PGTO,
          t1.QTDE_PARCELAS_ACORDO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) AS QTD_CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CASH_PRINCIPAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CASH,
          
            (SUM(t1.RAP)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS JUROS,
          
            (SUM(t1.DESCONTO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS DESCONTO,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RECEBIDO_PERF,
          t4.META AS META_CL1,
          t5.META AS META_CL2
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_MIS t2 ON (t1.DATA_BASE = t2.DATA_BASE) AND (t1.NUCONTA = t2.NUCONTA) AND
          (t1.PRODUTO = t2.PRODUTO)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_DU t3 ON (t1.DATA_PAGTO = t3.Data)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.METAS_MIS_CL1 t4 ON (t1.CL_STATUS = t4.CARTEIRA) AND (t1.DATA_BASE = t4.SAFRA)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.METAS_MIS_CL2 t5 ON (t1.CL_STATUS = t5.CARTEIRA) AND (t1.DATA_BASE = t5.SAFRA)
      WHERE t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO'
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.TIPO,
               (CALCULATED FX_ATRASO),
               (CALCULATED CL_STATUS),
               (CALCULATED FLAG_ENTRADA),
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.STATUS,
               t2.ORIGEM_AC,
               t2.APROVACAO,
               t1.RANGE_SALDO,
               t3.Du,
               t1.QTDE_PARCELAS_ACORDO,
               t4.META,
               t5.META;
QUIT;

/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 087 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

PROC SQL;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CAMPANHA_FLAG_REDIST_CRC AS
   SELECT t1.CONTRATO_2,
          t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.NuCpfCnpj,
          t1.ASSESS_CA,
          t1.SALDO_RISCO_CA,
          t1.'Valor Adicional (Remuneração)'n,
          
            (CASE WHEN t2.COUNT_of_contr = 2 THEN 'REDIST' ELSE 'UNICO' END) AS FLAG_REDISTRIBUICAO_CRC,
          t3.DT_REGULARIZADO,
          t4.DIAS_AT
      FROM WORK.MARCANDO_DUPLICIDASDES t2
           LEFT JOIN (WORK.BASE_CAMPANHA t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_PERF_CA t3 ON (t1.NUCONTA = t3.NUCONTA) AND (t1.PRODUTO = t3.PRODUTO) AND
          (t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = t3.COD_ASSESS)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_BASILEIA t4 ON (t1.NUCONTA = t4.NUCONTA) AND (t1.PRODUTO = t4.PRODUTO)) ON
          (t2.CONTRATO_2 = t1.CONTRATO_2);
QUIT;

/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.EFETIVACOES_CA_CONS);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.EFETIVACOES_CA_CONS AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.DATA_EFET),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.DATA_EFET),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          
            (DAY(t1.DATA_EFET)) AS DIA,
          t1.ESTADO_CONTA,
          
            (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA ='AVAL ADMINIS' THEN 'TOLEDO' ELSE t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA END) AS ASSESSORIA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'A.<_5'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 5 AND 30 THEN 'B.5-30'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 31 AND 60 THEN 'C.31-60'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 61  AND 74 THEN 'D.61-74'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO > 74 THEN 'D.>_74'
            ELSE ' VALIDAR'
            END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.ATRASO < 5  THEN 'A.<_5'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 5 AND 15 THEN 'B.5-15'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 16 AND 30 THEN 'C.16-30'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 31  AND 45 THEN 'D.31-45'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 46  AND 60 THEN 'E.46-60'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO BETWEEN 61  AND 70 THEN 'F.61-70'
            WHEN t1.ATRASO > 70 THEN 'G.>_70'
            ELSE ' VALIDAR'
            END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO_GRAN,
          
            (SUM(t1.TAXA)) FORMAT=8.2 AS TAXA,
          t1.QTDE_PARCELAS,
          
            (COUNT((INPUT(CATS(PUT(PRODUTO,Z2.),PUT(NUCONTA,Z10.)),Z12.)))) AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_ENTRADA)) FORMAT=8.2 AS VALOR_ENTRADA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_IOF)) FORMAT=8.2 AS VALOR_IOF,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_ENTR_TOTAL)) FORMAT=8.2 AS VALOR_ENTR_TOTAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_PRINC)) FORMAT=8.2 AS VALOR_PRINC,
          
            (SUM(t1.VALOR_PMT)) FORMAT=8.2 AS VALOR_PMT
      FROM WORK.EFETIVACOES_CA t1
      WHERE (CALCULATED SAFRA) >= 202306 AND (CALCULATED ASSESSORIA) NOT IN
           (
           'BANCO BV NO','REALE ADVOGA','RR SERVICOS','ZANC SERVICO',
           )
      GROUP BY (CALCULATED SAFRA),
               (CALCULATED DIA),
               t1.ESTADO_CONTA,
               (CALCULATED ASSESSORIA),
               (CALCULATED FAIXA_ATRASO),
               (CALCULATED FAIXA_ATRASO_GRAN),
               t1.QTDE_PARCELAS;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 097 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 098 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_CA AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.SAFRA),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.SAFRA),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          
            (CASE WHEN t1.DU_ENVIO = . OR t1.DU_ENVIO IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE t1.DU_ENVIO END) AS DU_ENVIO_CART,
          t1.DsDiaSemana AS DIA_ENVIO,
          t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO < 4 THEN 'a.EM DIA'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 4 AND 15 then 'B.4 A 15'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 16  AND 30  then 'C.16 A 30'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 31  AND 45   then 'D.31 A 45'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 46   AND 60   then 'E.46 A 60'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 61   AND 70   then 'F.61 A 70'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO BETWEEN 70   AND 74   then 'G.70 A 74'
            WHEN t1.DIAS_ATRASO >  74   then 'H.MAIOR 74'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR' END
            ) AS FAIXA_QUEBRADA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA LABEL='',
          t1.TPCLASSE,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA < 100 THEN 'A.ATE_100'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 100 AND 250 THEN 'B.100_250'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 250 AND 500 THEN 'C.250_500'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 500  AND 1000 THEN 'D.500_1K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 1000  AND 2000 THEN 'E.1K_2K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 2000  AND 5000 THEN 'F.2K_5K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 5000  AND 10000 THEN 'G.5K_10K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 10000  AND 20000 THEN 'H.10K_20K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 20000  AND 50000 THEN 'I.20K_50K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 50000  AND 80000 THEN 'J.50K_80K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 80000  AND 100000 THEN 'K.80K_100K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 100000  AND 200000 THEN 'L.100K_200K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA BETWEEN 200000  AND 300000 THEN 'M.200K_300K'
            WHEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA > 300000 THEN 'N.MAIOR_300K'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR' END
            ) AS RANGE_SALDO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS COUNT_of_CHAVE,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=bestx15.2 AS SUM_of_VLR_COBRANCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO)) FORMAT=bestx15.2 AS SUM_of_VLR_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.SAFRA < '01JUL2024'D THEN t1.PILOTO_DIGITAL ELSE 'N' END) AS FLAG_PILOTO_DIG
      FROM WORK.PERF_CA t1
      GROUP BY (CALCULATED SAFRA),
               (CALCULATED DU_ENVIO_CART),
               t1.DsDiaSemana,
               t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
               (CALCULATED FAIXA_QUEBRADA),
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.TPCLASSE,
               (CALCULATED RANGE_SALDO),
               (CALCULATED FLAG_PILOTO_DIG);
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_B_RESULTADO_CL_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.DU_PGTO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA_AC IS NOT NULL THEN 'DIG' ELSE 'TRAD' END) AS ORIGEM_AC,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) AS CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RECEBIDO_PERF
      FROM WORK.QUERY_FOR_B_RESULTADO_CL t1
      WHERE t1.DU_PGTO NOT IS MISSING AND t1.ASSESSORIA NOT IN
           (
           '',
           'VILELA IBANEZ',
           '#N/A'
           )
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.DU_PGTO,
               (CALCULATED ORIGEM_AC),
               t1.FX_ATRASO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;

          t1.DIA_PROD_AC,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_PG_ENTRADA) / COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS 'ENTRADA_MÉDIA'n
      FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_1015_PAGOS_MENSAL t1

               t1.DIA_PROD_AC;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ORACLE AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t2.FX_ATRASO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_CONTRATO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SALDO_CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VL_PG_ENTRADA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VL_PG_ENTRADA
      FROM WORK.BASE_ORACLE_2024 t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.FAIXA_ATRASO_RESULT t2 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t2.CONTRATO) AND (t1.SAFRA = t2.DATA_BASE)
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t2.FX_ATRASO;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_B_RESULTADO_CL_0002 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          
            (case
            when t1.RECEBIDO = 50 then '50 reais' else 'maior50reais' end ) AS FLAG_50_REAIS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) AS QTD_ACORDOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS RECEBIDO_PERF,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS RECEBIDO
      FROM WORK.B_RESULTADO_CL t1
      WHERE t1.DATA_BASE IN
           (
           202406,
           202407
           ) AND t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO'
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               (CALCULATED FLAG_50_REAIS),
               t1.ASSESSORIA
      ORDER BY t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL DESC,
               t1.RECEBIDO;
QUIT;

/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 110 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.B_VALIDA_ENTRADA_BI_FINAL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.B_VALIDA_ENTRADA_BI_FINAL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.CONTRATO LABEL='',
          t1.NU_ACORDO,
          t1.TIPO,
          
            (CASE
            when t1.FX_ATRASO = '75 A 120'  then '1.75 A 120'
            when t1.FX_ATRASO = '121 A 180'  then '2.121 A 180'
            when t1.FX_ATRASO = '181 A 360'  then '3.181 A 360'
            when t1.FX_ATRASO = '361 A 720'  then '4.361 A 720'
            when t1.FX_ATRASO = 'ACIMA DE 720' then '5.ACIMA DE 720'
            ELSE ''
            END) AS FX_ATRASO,
          t1.DT_PGTO_ENTRADA LABEL='',
          
            (DAY(DATEPART(t1.DT_PGTO_ENTRADA) )) AS DIA_PGTO_ENTRADA,
          t1.VL_PG_ENTRADA FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS VL_PG_ENTRADA,
          t1.IOF FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS IOF,
          t1.FLAG_ISENTO LABEL='',
          t1.DT_PROX_PARC LABEL='',
          t1.VL_PARC FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS VL_PARC,
          t1.COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.ASSESSORIA LABEL='',
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%BELLIN%') THEN
            'BELLINATI'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%AVAL%') THEN 'TOLEDO'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%ANGELIS%') THEN
            'LM ANGELIS'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%NOVA%') THEN
            'LM ANGELIS'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%RN%') THEN 'RN ADV'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%PASCH%') THEN 'RN ADV'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <>  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA <> 4041 AND ASSESSORIA LIKE('%CONCILIG%') THEN
            'CONCILIG'
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA =  4040 AND t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = 4041 THEN 'VU'
            ELSE 'OUTROS'
            END
            ) AS ASSESSORIA2,
          t1.PERC_PARC LABEL='',
          t1.SALDO_CONTRATO FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS SALDO_CONTRATO,
          t1.RISCO_TOTAL FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RISCO_TOTAL_CART,
          t1.AHAUTCOLLID LABEL='',
          t1.DESC_PRINC FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS DESC_PRINC,
          t1.FLAG_STATUS_ENTRADA LABEL='',
          t1.PGTO_PARC_POST FORMAT=BESTX15.2 LABEL='' AS PGTO_PARC_POST,
          t1.COND_ATUAL_AC LABEL='',
          
            (CASE
            when t1.FX_ATRASO IN ('75 A 120','121 A 180','181 A 360')  then 'CL1'
            when t1.FX_ATRASO IN ('361 A 720','ACIMA DE 720')  then 'CL2'
            ELSE ''
            END) AS FASE
      FROM WORK.B_VALIDA_ENTRADA_BI t1;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ENTRANTES_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.DTENVIO,
          
            (t1.DTENVIO + 1) FORMAT=CATDFDD10. AS DATA_ENTRADA,
          
            (DAY(t1.DTENVIO +1)) AS DIA_ENTRADA,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS QTD,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VR_SALDO_TOTAL
      FROM WORK.CART_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.TIPO,
               t1.CL_STATUS,
               t1.DTENVIO,
               (CALCULATED DATA_ENTRADA),
               (CALCULATED DIA_ENTRADA),
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ENTRANTES_CL_BI AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.DIA_ENTRADA,
          t2.Du AS DU_ENTRADA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DATA_BASE < 202510 AND t1.FX_ATRASO LIKE('%75%') THEN t1.FX_ATRASO
            WHEN t1.DATA_BASE >= 202510 AND t1.FX_ATRASO LIKE('%75%') THEN '95 A 120'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END

            ) AS FX_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.QTD,
          t1.VR_SALDO_TOTAL,
          
            (t1.VR_SALDO_TOTAL / t1.QTD) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS 'TICKET_MÉDIO_ENTRADA'n
      FROM WORK.BASE_ENTRANTES_CL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_DU t2 ON (t1.DATA_ENTRADA = t2.Data);
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA_CA AS
   SELECT t1.safra,
          t1.data_extracao,
          t1.CdAssessoria,
          t1.NmAssessoria,
          
            (day(t1.data_extracao) ) AS DIA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NuContrato)) AS QTD_CONTRATO,
          
            (case
            when t1.NuDiasAtraso <= 30 then 'a.Ate_30'
            when t1.NuDiasAtraso > 30 then 'b.Maior_30'
            else 'Validar'
            END) AS ATRASO_FAIXA_CONS,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso < 5  THEN 'A.ATE 5'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  5 AND 10  THEN 'B.5 A 10'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  10 AND 15  THEN 'C.10 A 15'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  15 AND 20  THEN 'D.15 A 20'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  20 AND 25  THEN 'E.20 A 25'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  25 AND 30  THEN 'F.25 A 30'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  30 AND 40  THEN 'G.30 A 40'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  40 AND 50  THEN 'H.40 A 50'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  50 AND 60  THEN 'I.50 A 60'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  60 AND 70  THEN 'J.60 A 70'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso BETWEEN  71 AND 73  THEN 'J.61 A 73'
            WHEN t1.NuDiasAtraso >73  THEN 'K.MAIOR 73'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO
      FROM WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA t1
      WHERE t1.CARTEIRA = 'CA'
      GROUP BY t1.safra,
               t1.data_extracao,
               t1.CdAssessoria,
               t1.NmAssessoria,
               (CALCULATED DIA),
               (CALCULATED ATRASO_FAIXA_CONS),
               (CALCULATED FAIXA_ATRASO);
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 117 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 118 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.TAB_CARTEIRA_CONS_BI_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.TAB_CARTEIRA_CONS_BI_CA AS
   SELECT t1.safra,
          t1.data_extracao,
          t1.CdAssessoria,
          t1.NmAssessoria,
          t1.DIA,
          t1.QTD_CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t2.QTD_CONTRATOS)) AS QTD_CONT_FLUXO,
          t1.ATRASO_FAIXA_CONS,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO
      FROM WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA_CA t1
           LEFT JOIN CRIS.BASE_FLUXO t2 ON (t1.FAIXA_ATRASO = t2.FAIXA_ATRASO) AND (t1.safra = t2.SAFRA_FLUXO) AND
          (t1.DIA = t2.DIA_ENVIO) AND (t1.CdAssessoria = t2.CD_ASSESSORIA) AND (t1.ATRASO_FAIXA_CONS = t2.FAIXA)
      GROUP BY t1.safra,
               t1.data_extracao,
               t1.CdAssessoria,
               t1.NmAssessoria,
               t1.DIA,
               t1.QTD_CONTRATO,
               t1.ATRASO_FAIXA_CONS,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_EFETIVACOES_CA_HIST AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.DATA_EFET),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.DATA_EFET),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS SAFRA_AC,
          t1.DATA_EFET,
          t1.ATRASO,
          t1.NUCONTA,
          t1.PRODUTO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%TOLEDO%') THEN 4001
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%AVAL %') THEN 4001
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%BELLINATI%') THEN 4006
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%CRC%') THEN 4007
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%CONCILIG%') THEN 4062
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%PILOTO%') THEN 4010
            WHEN t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA LIKE('%PASCHOAL%') THEN 4005
            ELSE 99999
            END) AS COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.NOME_ASSESSORIA,
          
            (CATS(t1.NUCONTA,t1.PRODUTO)) AS CHAVE,
          t1.ESTADO_CONTA,
          t1.DATA_PGTO_ENTRADA,
          t1.DATA_VENC_1PMT,
          t1.VALOR_ENTRADA,
          t1.VALOR_IOF,
          t1.VALOR_ENTR_TOTAL,
          t1.VALOR_PRINC,
          t1.VALOR_PMT,
          t1.QTDE_PARCELAS,
          t1.TAXA,
          t1.VALOR_JUROS,
          t1.FAIXA_CS,
          t1.VALOR_COB,
          t1.SEMANA,
          t1.STATUS_ATRASO,
          t1.SAFRA,
          t1.TIPO_PROMESSA,
          t1.MISSING,
          
            (cats(t1.NUCONTA,t1.PRODUTO)) AS CONC
      FROM WORK.EFETIVACOES_CA t1
      WHERE (CALCULATED SAFRA_AC) >= 202301;
QUIT;
