PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_RESULTADO_CA_HIST AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA_RESULT,
          t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.NUCONTA,
          t1.PRODUTO,
          t1.COD_ASSESS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.VLR_COBRANCA,
          t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t2.SAFRA_AC IS NOT NULL AND t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO <> . THEN 'ACORDO'
            WHEN t2.SAFRA_AC IS NULL AND t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO <> . THEN 'OUTROS'
            ELSE '' END) AS TIPO_NEGOCIACAO
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADO_CA_HIST t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_EFETIVACOES_CA_HIST t2 ON (t1.NUCONTA = t2.NUCONTA) AND (t1.PRODUTO = t2.PRODUTO) AND
          (t1.COD_ASSESS = t2.COD_ASSESSORIA) AND (t1.SAFRA_RESULT = t2.SAFRA_AC);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_B_ACORDOS AS
   SELECT t1.DTINICIO,
          t1.DTFIM,
          t1.CDAGENTE,
          t1.NMAGENTE,
          t1.PLANO,
          t1.NUPARCELA,
          t1.VRPARCELA,
          t1.NUACORDO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRACORDO)) FORMAT=BEST8. AS SUM_of_VRACORDO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRPARCELA * t1.NUPARCELA)) AS VLR_BAIXADO
      FROM WORK.B_ACORDOS t1
      GROUP BY t1.DTINICIO,
               t1.DTFIM,
               t1.CDAGENTE,
               t1.NMAGENTE,
               t1.PLANO,
               t1.NUPARCELA,
               t1.VRPARCELA,
               t1.NUACORDO;
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ACORDOS_FINAL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ACORDOS_FINAL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.NUACORDO,
          t1.PLANO,
          t1.PMTQUEBRA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DTQUEBRAACORDO <> . AND  UPCASE(t1.DSMTVOQUEBRA) LIKE('%FALTA%') THEN 'QUEBRA_PGTO'
            WHEN t1.DTQUEBRAACORDO <> . AND  UPCASE(t1.DSMTVOQUEBRA) LIKE ('%ACORDO%') THEN 'NOVA_PROPOSTA'
            WHEN t1.DTQUEBRAACORDO = .  THEN 'SEM_QUEBRA'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS MOTIVO_QUEBRA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DTQUITACAO <> .  AND t1.DTQUEBRA = . THEN 'QUITADO'
            WHEN t1.DTQUITACAO = .  AND t1.DTQUEBRA <> . THEN 'QUEBRADO'
            WHEN t1.DTQUITACAO = .  AND t1.DTQUEBRA = . THEN 'ATIVO'

            ELSE 'VALIDAR' END) AS SITUACAO_AC,
          t1.VRACORDO,
          t1.NUPARCELA,

          t1.FLQUEBRAMANUAL
      FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_FINAL t1;

/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_COLCHAO_REAL_0001);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_COLCHAO_REAL_0001 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT t1.SAFRA_AC,
          t1.DT_EFT_AC,
          t1.DT_FINAL,
          t1.SAFRA_FIM,
          t1.COD_ACORDO,
          t1.CONTRATO,
          t1.COD_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.PLANO,
          t1.VLR_PARC,
          t1.VLR_ACORDO,
          t1.STATUS_ACORDO,
          t1.OBSERVACAO_AC,
          t1.ULTIM_PARC_PAGA,
          t1.DIA_AC,
          t1.ULTIM_ATUAL,
          t1.STATUS_PARC,
          t1.CONDICAO_AC,
          t4.DSFASE_3 AS DSFASE,
          
            (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL1" then 0.19
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL2" then 0.20
            ELSE 0
            end) AS ATINGIMENTO_100,
          
            (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL1" then 0.10
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL2" then 0.11
            ELSE 0
            end) AS ATINGIMENTO_MIN,
          
            (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL1" then 0.22
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL2" then 0.23
            ELSE 0
            end) AS ATINGIMENTO_MAX,
          
            (t1.VLR_PARC * (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3="CL1" then 0.19
            when t4.DSFASE_3="CL2" then 0.20
            ELSE 0
            end)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CUSTO_ATING_100,
          
            (t1.VLR_PARC * (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL1" then 0.10
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL2" then 0.11
            ELSE 0
            end)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CUSTO_ATING_MIN,
          
            (t1.VLR_PARC * (case
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL1" then 0.22
            when t4.DSFASE_3 ="CL2" then 0.23
            ELSE 0
            end)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CUSTO_ATING_MAX,
          
            (case
            when t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = 1015 and t3.ASSESSORIA ='LM ANGELIS' then t3.CL_STATUS
            else 0 end) AS TRAT_DIGITAL,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = 1015 AND (case
            when t1.COD_ASSESSORIA = 1015 and t3.ASSESSORIA ='LM ANGELIS' then t3.CL_STATUS
            else 0 end) = 0 THEM 'RETIRAR'

            ELSE 'MANTER'
            END) AS VALIDACAO_AC_DIGITAL
      FROM WORK.BASE_COLCHAO_REAL_LM t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.B_COLCHAO_LM t2 ON (t1.COD_ACORDO = t2.NUACORDO)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.B_IDENT_ASSE_LM t3 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t3.CONTRATO) AND (t1.SAFRA_AC = t3.DATA_BASE)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.B_CORR_AC_LM t4 ON (t1.COD_ACORDO = t4.CDACORDO)
      ORDER BY t1.COD_ACORDO;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 134 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 135 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_CARTAO AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.DT_ARQUIVO,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.COD_ASSESS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.VLR_COBRANCA,
          t2.PROD,
          t2.CONTRATO,
          t2.VALOR_DE_RISCO,
          
            (Case
             when SAFRA is not null and PROD is null then 1 else 0 end ) AS Nao_consid_crc,
          
            (Case
            when SAFRA is null and PROD is not null then 1 else 0 end ) AS Consid_errado_CRC
      FROM WORK.BASE_CARTAO t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_MES_BV_202409_0000 t2 ON (t1.CHAVE = t2.CONTRATO) AND (t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = t2.PROD);
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 139 */

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_REM_CL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_REM_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(t1.PRODUTO,Z2.),PUT(t1.NUCONTA,Z10.)),Z12.)) AS CONTRATO,
          t1.CL,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_ENVIADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_SALDO_ENVIADO,
          t1.FAIXA_ATR_AC,
          t1.RECEBIDO_BV,
          t1.remuneracao
      FROM WORK.BASE_REM_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               (CALCULATED CONTRATO),
               t1.CL,
               t1.FAIXA_ATR_AC,
               t1.RECEBIDO_BV,
               t1.remuneracao;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.TAB_DESCONTO AS
   SELECT 
            (input(cats(put(year((t1.DtMovTransacao-1)),z4.),put(month((t1.DtMovTransacao -1)),z2.)),z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          t1.CONTRATO,
          
            (SUM(t1.CreditoLocal)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_CreditoLocal
      FROM WORK.DESCONTO_ANALITICO_2024 t1
      GROUP BY (CALCULATED SAFRA),
               t1.CONTRATO;
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_ANALISE_ACORDOS);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_ANALISE_ACORDOS AS
   SELECT t1.DTINICIO,
          t1.DTCRIACAO,
          t1.NUACORDO,
          t1.CDAGENTE,
          t1.NMAGENTE,
          t1.VRACORDO,
          t1.DsFase,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.A_Acordo BETWEEN 75 AND 120 THEN '75 A 120'
            WHEN t1.A_Acordo BETWEEN 121 AND 180 THEN '121 A 180'
            WHEN t1.A_Acordo BETWEEN 181 AND 360 THEN '181 A 360'
            WHEN t1.A_Acordo BETWEEN 361 AND 720 THEN '361 A 720'
            WHEN t1.A_Acordo > 720 THEN 'ACIMA 720'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR'
            END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO_AC,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.CDAGENTE = 1015 THEN 'DIGITAL' ELSE 'TRADICIONAL'
            END) AS ORIGEM,
          t1.VRPARCELA
      FROM WORK.ANALISE_ACORDOS t1
      WHERE t1.DTCRIACAO BETWEEN t1.DTINICIO AND t1.DTFIM AND t1.DTPGTO NOT IS MISSING AND t1.DTPGTO NOT = .;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 149 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 150 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_ANALISE_ACORDOS_0000);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_ANALISE_ACORDOS_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.DTINICIO,
          t1.DsFase,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO_AC,
          t1.ORIGEM,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NUACORDO)) AS COUNT_of_NUACORDO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRACORDO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VRACORDO1,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRPARCELA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VRPARCELA
      FROM WORK.QUERY_FOR_ANALISE_ACORDOS t1
      GROUP BY t1.DTINICIO,
               t1.DsFase,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO_AC,
               t1.ORIGEM;

/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ESFORCO_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ESFORCO_CA AS
   SELECT t1.safra,
          t1.Du,
          t1.DsDiaSemana,
          t1.NmAssessoria,
          
            (SUM(t1.qtd_contratos_disc)/SUM(t1.qtd_contratos_cart)) AS PENETRACAO_LISTA,
          
            (SUM(t1.TOTAL_ATENDIMENTOS) /SUM(t1.TENTATIVAS_TOTAIS_C_RING)) AS HITRATE,
          
            ((SUM(t1.TOTAL_COM_CPC_CPCA) + SUM(t1.PROMESSA))/SUM(t1.TOTAL_ATENDIMENTOS)) AS CPC,
          
            (SUM(t1.PROMESSA) /(SUM(t1.PROMESSA) + SUM(t1.TOTAL_COM_CPC_CPCA))) AS PROMESSAS,
          
            (SUM(t1.ATENDIMENTO_DIGITAL)/SUM(t1.TOTAL_ATENDIMENTOS)) AS ATEND_DIGITAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.TELEFONES_DISTINTOS)/SUM(t1.TENTATIVAS_TOTAIS_C_RING)
            ) AS TEL_DISTINTOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.WHATSAPP) /SUM(t1.TENTATIVAS_TOTAIS_C_RING)) AS WHATSUP
      FROM WORK.BASE_ESFORCO_CA t1
      GROUP BY t1.safra,
               t1.Du,
               t1.DsDiaSemana,
               t1.NmAssessoria;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 175 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 176 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ANA_0000);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ANA_0000 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT t1.TIPO,
          t1.PBDTCICLO,
          t1.DT_PAGTO_BOL,
          t1.DT_VENCIMENTO,
          t1.CONTRATO,
          t1.Assessoria,
          t2.CD_ASSESSORIA,
          
            (CASE WHEN t4.DMACCT IS NOT NULL THEN "ATIVO_CYBER_20241216"
            ELSE ''
            END) AS CONTRATO_ATIVO,
          t2.AGFIRM AS ULTI_ASSESSORIA,
          t2.DT_DISPONIBILIZACAO AS ULT_DISTRIBUICAO,
          t3.AHDT AS DT_ACORDO_ATIVO,
          t3.AHID AS COD_ACORDO_ATIVO,
          t3.AHPRD AS PLANO_AC,
          t3.AHSTATUS AS STATUS_AC_CYBER,
          t4.DMAMTDLQ AS SALDO_ATIVO_CYBER,
          t1.COD_ASS_ACORDO,
          t1.NRO_ACORDO,
          t1.NN,
          t1.PLANO_PARC,
          t1.NRO_PARCELA,
          t1.VL_BOLETO,
          t1.VL_PAGAMENTO,
          t1.VL_BAIXA_SEM_HONO_SEM_IOF,
          t1.VL_HONORARIO_DEVIDO,
          t1.VL_HONORARIO_PAGO,
          t1.VL_IOF,
          t1.TIPO_IOF,
          t1.NOME_ARQ,
          t1.ORIGEM_PAGTO,
          t1.STATUS_PAGTO_HONO,
          t1.STATUS_ACORDO,
          t1.MOTIVO_REJEICAO,
          t1.DT_QUEBRA_ACORDO,
          t1.MOTIVO_QUEBRA_ACORDO,
          t1.QUEBRA_NO_DIA_DO_RECEBTO,
          t1.SALDO_EM_COBRANCA,
          t1.ACORDO_EM_ANDAMENTO,
          t1.VL_BOL_PROX_PARC_ACOR_ANDAM,
          t1.ULT_ACORDO_QUEBRADO,
          t1.VL_BOL_PROX_PARC_ACOR_QUEB
      FROM WORK.BASE_ANA t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_CYBER_ANA t2 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t2.NR_CONTRATO) AND (t1.CONTRATO = t2.NR_CONTRATO)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.ACORDOS_ATIVOS_ANA t3 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t3.AHACCT) AND (t1.CONTRATO = t3.AHACCT)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.CONTRATOS_ATIVOS_ANA t4 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t4.DMACCT) AND (t1.CONTRATO = t4.DMACCT);
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_MAIOR_SALDO_ATIVOS AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.CONTRATO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (MAX(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS MAIOR_SALDO
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL t1
      WHERE t1.DATA_PAGTO NOT = . AND t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO'
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.TIPO,
               t1.CONTRATO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.ASSESSORIA
      ORDER BY t1.DATA_BASE DESC,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL DESC;
QUIT;

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.TAB_RESULTADO_CART_CA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.TAB_RESULTADO_CART_CA AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.SAFRA),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.SAFRA),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS SAFRA_REF,
          t1.TPCLASSE,
          t1.COD_ASSESS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.SAFRA >= '01MAR2024'D AND t1.ASSESSORIA IN('CRC','CONCILIG') THEN 'PILOTO_PA_FIXA'
            WHEN  t1.ASSESSORIA IN('PILOTO_BOM') THEN 'PILOTO_DIGITAL'
             ELSE 'MODELO_ATUAL'
            END) AS TIPO_REMUNERACAO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.VLR_COBRANCA,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.DT_REGULARIZADO <> .  THEN t1.VLR_COBRANCA
            ELSE 0
            END) AS VLR_REG
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADO_CA t1
      WHERE (CALCULATED SAFRA_REF) >= 202301;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 188 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 189 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_CONSOLIDADA);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CONSOLIDADA AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA_REF,
          t2.ULTIMO_DU,
          t1.TPCLASSE,
          t1.COD_ASSESS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.TIPO_REMUNERACAO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          
            (ROUND((CASE
            WHEN t1.TPCLASSE ='E' THEN COUNT(t1.CHAVE)
            WHEN t1.TPCLASSE ='F' THEN COUNT(t1.CHAVE) /t2.ULTIMO_DU
            ELSE .
            END) , 2)) AS CONTRATOS_FINAL,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_REG)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.TAB_RESULTADO_CART_CA t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.TAB_DU_MES t2 ON (t1.SAFRA_REF = t2.SAFRA_DU)
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA_REF,
               t2.ULTIMO_DU,
               t1.TPCLASSE,
               t1.COD_ASSESS,
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.TIPO_REMUNERACAO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 189 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CP_GOE_CONSOLIDADA AS
   SELECT t1.safra,
          t1.Du,
          t1.DsDiaSemana,
          t1.DT_CRIACAO_BASE,
          t1.TP_ENVIO,
          t1.NM_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.TP_PESSOA,
          t1.TPPRODUTO,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          
            (SUM(t1.CARTEIRA)) AS CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_RISCO)) AS VLR_RISCO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ACIONA)) AS ACIONA,
          
            (SUM(t1.ALO)) AS ALO,
          
            (SUM(t1.CPC)) AS CPC,
          
            (SUM(t1.PROMESSA)) AS PROMESSA
      FROM WORK.BASE_CP_GOE t1

               t1.Du,

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CP_GOE_CONSOLIDADA AS
   SELECT t1.safra,
          t1.Du,
          t1.DsDiaSemana,
          t1.DT_CRIACAO_BASE,
          t1.TP_ENVIO,
          t1.NM_ASSESSORIA,
          t1.TP_PESSOA,
          t1.TPPRODUTO,
          t1.FAIXA_ATRASO,
          
            (SUM(t1.CARTEIRA)) AS CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_RISCO)) AS VLR_RISCO,
          
            (SUM(t1.ACIONA)) AS ACIONA,
          
            (SUM(t1.ALO)) AS ALO,
          
            (SUM(t1.CPC)) AS CPC,
          
            (SUM(t1.PROMESSA)) AS PROMESSA
      FROM WORK.BASE_CP_GOE t1
      WHERE t1.NM_ASSESSORIA NOT IN
           (
           'CARTAO_CL_BELLINATI_VU',
           'CARTAO_CL_TOLEDO_VU',
           'FLEX_CL',
           'NO_ACTION_BV',
           'SERVICES_CL',
           'VILELA_CL'
           )
      GROUP BY t1.safra,
               t1.Du,
               t1.DsDiaSemana,
               t1.DT_CRIACAO_BASE,
               t1.TP_ENVIO,
               t1.NM_ASSESSORIA,
               t1.TP_PESSOA,
               t1.TPPRODUTO,
               t1.FAIXA_ATRASO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL_2 AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.COD_ASSESS LABEL='',
          t1.ASSESSORIA LABEL='',
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS COUNT_of_CHAVE,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VLR_COBRANCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VLR_REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL_2 t1
      WHERE t1.SAFRA = '1Feb2025'd
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.COD_ASSESS,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_PGTO);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_PGTO AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.COD_ASSESS LABEL='',
          t1.ASSESSORIA LABEL='',
          
            (COUNT(cats("'",PUT(t1.CHAVE,Z13.),"'"))) AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VLR_COBRANCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VLR_REGULARIZADO
      FROM WORK.CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL_2 t1
      WHERE t1.SAFRA = '1Oct2025'd AND t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO NOT IS MISSING
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.COD_ASSESS,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 207 */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CONSOLIDADA_FLUXO_CA AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA_FLUXO,
          t1.Du,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.ASSESSORIA ='AVAL_CL' THEN 'TOLEDO' ELSE t1.ASSESSORIA
            END) AS ASSESSORIA_CORR,
          t1.ATRASO_RECEBIDO_ASS LABEL='',
          t1.CART LABEL='',
          t1.DIA_RECEBIDO_ASS LABEL='',
          
            (SUM(t1.QTD)) FORMAT=BEST15X.2 AS QTD,
          
            (SUM(t1.VL_DIVIDA)) FORMAT=BEST15X.2 AS SUM_of_VL_NOVA_DIVIDA
      FROM WORK.BASE_DIARIA_CA t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA_FLUXO,
               t1.Du,
               (CALCULATED ASSESSORIA_CORR),
               t1.ATRASO_RECEBIDO_ASS,
               t1.CART,
               t1.DIA_RECEBIDO_ASS
      ORDER BY t1.SAFRA_FLUXO,
               t1.DIA_RECEBIDO_ASS;
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_CARTEIRA_0000);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_CARTEIRA_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.cd_contrato,
          t1.dt_criacao_base,
          
            (case
            when t1.dt_criacao_base ='01apr2025'd then 'ESTOQUE' ELSE 'FLUXO'
            END) AS STATUS_CART,
          t1.cd_assessoria,
          t1.nm_assessoria,
          t1.qt_dias_atraso,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.qt_dias_atraso <= 120 THEN '75 A 120'
            WHEN t1.qt_dias_atraso BETWEEN 121 AND 180 THEN '121 A 180'
            WHEN t1.qt_dias_atraso BETWEEN 181 AND 360 THEN '181 A 360'
            WHEN t1.qt_dias_atraso BETWEEN 361 AND 720 THEN '361 A 720'
            WHEN t1.qt_dias_atraso > 720 THEN 'ACIMA 720'
            ELSE 'VALIDAR' END) AS FAIXA_ATRASO,
          t1.vr_risco_contrato,
          t1.ds_produto_contrato,
          t1.fl_contrato_suspenso,
          t1.cd_tipo_suspensao,
          t1.row_num
      FROM WORK.BASE_CARTEIRA t1;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 214 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_PGTO_CYBER_CRIS AS
   SELECT t1.APAHID,
          t1.APDETID,
          t1.APINDATE,
          t1.APDUEDT,
          t1.APAMT,
          t1.APAMTPAY,
          t1.APFLAG,
          t1.APVLIOFD,
          t1.APVLIOFA,
          t1.APVLIOF,
          t1.AHACCT,
          t2.CDACORDO AS CDACORDO_MIS_COB,
          t2.PARCELA_PAGA AS PARCELA_PAGA_MIS_COB,
          t2.RECEBIDO_BV AS RECEBIDO_BV_MIS_COB,
          t3.CDACORDO AS CDACORDO_MIS_CYBER,
          t3.PARCELA_PAGA AS PARCELA_PAGA_MIS_CYBER,
          t3.RECEBIDO_BV AS RECEBIDO_BV_MIS_CYBER
      FROM WORK.BASE_PGTO_CYBER_CRIS t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_PGTOS_MIS_COB t2 ON (t1.APAHID = t2.CDACORDO)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_PGTOS_MIS_CYBER t3 ON (t1.APAHID = t3.CDACORDO);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_RESULTADOS_202404 AS

          t1.RECEBIDO_PERF,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.RECEBIDO_PERF /t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL < 0.30 THEN 'ERRO' ELSE 'OK' END) AS VALIDA_REGRA_PARC_CL1
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_202404_CL t1

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_RESULTADOS_2_0000 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_PERF
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADOS_202404_CL t1
      WHERE t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO' AND t1.ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO' AND t1.GP_STIR_SHAKEN NOT IN
           (
           'TESTE 0303',
           'TESTE STIR SHAKEN'
           )
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.TIPO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_RESULTADO_CL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_RESULTADO_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.DATA_PAGTO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t3.DMACCT <> . THEN 'REDIST' ELSE 'NORMAL' END) AS FLAG_REDIST_TOLEDO,
          t2.Du,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_PERF
      FROM WORK.BASE_RESULTADO_CL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.DUS_CL t2 ON (t1.DATA_PAGTO = t2.Data)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.CART_REDIST_TOLEDO t3 ON (t1.CONTRATO = t3.DMACCT)
      WHERE t1.GP_STIR_SHAKEN NOT IN
           (
           'TESTE 0303',
           'TESTE STIR SHAKEN'
           )
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.CL_STATUS,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.ASSESSORIA,
               t1.DATA_PAGTO,
               (CALCULATED FLAG_REDIST_TOLEDO),
               t2.Du;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 222 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 223 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_RESULTADO_CL1 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,

            when t1.RECEBIDO < 50 then 'menor 50 reais' else 'maior 50 reais' end) AS FLAG_ENTRADA,
          t1.FLAG_IOF,
          t1.GP_STIR_SHAKEN,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          t1.RECEBIDO_PERF

/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL AS
   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.SAFRA),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.SAFRA),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS SAFRA,
          
            (DAY(t1.DTENVIO)) AS DIA_ENVIO,
          t1.DU_ENVIO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.ASSESSORIA LABEL='',
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS COUNT_of_CHAVE1,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_VLR_COBRANCA
      FROM WORK.CARTEIRA_AMIGAVEL t1
      WHERE t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW = 'FLUXO'
      GROUP BY (CALCULATED SAFRA),
               (CALCULATED DIA_ENVIO),
               t1.DU_ENVIO,
               t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 230 */

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_N_DIGITAL_OK AS
SELECT
A.*,
B.CHAVE AS REDIST
FROM BASE_DIGITAL A
LEFT JOIN BASE_N_DIGITAL B
ON A.CHAVE = B.CHAVE
 WHERE monotonic() <= 15
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CL1_SEM_DUP AS

          t1.CL_STATUS,

          t1.ASSESSORIA,

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_2025_ENTRANTES_CA AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA_FLUXO,
          
            (SUM(t1.qtd) / COUNT(t1.dia)) AS MEDIA_DIA,
          
            (COUNT(t1.dia)) AS COUNT_of_dia,
          
            (SUM(t1.qtd)) FORMAT=20. AS SUM_of_qtd
      FROM WORK.BASE_2025_ENTRANTES_CA t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA_FLUXO;
QUIT;

   CREATE TABLE WORK.CART_TOTAL_75_95 AS

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_CART_TOTAL_75_95 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t5.MIN_of_DTENVIO AS DTENVIO,
          t4.DIAS_AT AS DIAS_AT_ENTRADA,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.SUM_of_VR_SALDO_ATUAL AS VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          t2.DATA_PAGTO,
          t3.SUM_of_RECEBIDO_PERF AS RECEBIDO_PERF
      FROM WORK.CART_TOTAL_75_95 t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.PRIM_PGTO_MES t2 ON (t1.DATA_BASE = t2.DATA_BASE) AND (t1.CHAVE = t2.CHAVE)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.PAGTOS_CONSO t3 ON (t1.DATA_BASE = t3.DATA_BASE) AND (t1.CHAVE = t3.CHAVE)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.TAB_MENOR_ATRASO t4 ON (t1.DATA_BASE = t4.DATA_BASE) AND (t1.CHAVE = t4.CHAVE)
           LEFT JOIN WORK.TAB_DATA_ENVIO t5 ON (t1.DATA_BASE = t5.DATA_BASE) AND (t1.CHAVE = t5.CHAVE);
QUIT;

/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.TAB_RESULTADO_75_95);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.TAB_RESULTADO_75_95 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.DTENVIO,
          t1.DIAS_AT_ENTRADA,
          t1.CHAVE,
          t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          t1.DATA_PAGTO,
          t1.RECEBIDO_PERF,
          
            (t1.DATA_PAGTO-t1.DTENVIO + t1.DIAS_AT_ENTRADA) AS ATRASO_NO_PAGTO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN (t1.DATA_PAGTO-t1.DTENVIO + t1.DIAS_AT_ENTRADA) = . THEN ''
            WHEN  (t1.DATA_PAGTO-t1.DTENVIO + t1.DIAS_AT_ENTRADA) <= 95 THEN 'PGTO_ATE_95'
             ELSE 'PGTO_APOS_95'
            END) AS STATUS_PGTO_ATRASO
      FROM WORK.QUERY_FOR_CART_TOTAL_75_95 t1;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 250 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

   SELECT 
            (INPUT(CATS(PUT(YEAR(t1.SAFRA),Z4.),PUT(MONTH(t1.SAFRA),Z2.)),Z6.)) AS ANOMES,

      FROM WORK.BASE_REG_CA t1

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.CONS_RESULTADO_CL AS
   SELECT DISTINCT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.TIPO,
          t2.Du AS DU_PGTO,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.STATUS,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS CARTEIRA,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RECEBIDO_PERF,
          
            (SUM(t1.DESCONTO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS DESCONTO
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL t1
           LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_DU t2 ON (t1.DATA_PAGTO = t2.Data)
      WHERE t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO'
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.TIPO,
               t2.Du,
               t1.CL_STATUS,
               t1.STATUS,
               t1.FX_ATRASO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_0001 AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.DATA_PAGTO AS DATA_PAGTO,
          
            (MEAN(t1.QTDE_PARCELAS_ACORDO)) FORMAT=BEST2. AS PLANO_MEDIO,
          
            (CASE
            WHEN t1.APROVADOR IS NOT MISSING THEN 'EXCECAO' ELSE 'ALCADA' END ) AS TIPO_APROVACAO,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CDACORDO)) AS QTD,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS RECEBIDO_BV,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_ENVIADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SALDO_ENVIADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.DESCONTO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS DESCONTO,
          
            (SUM(t1.VRPRINCIPALNEGOCIACAO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS VRPRINCIPALNEGOCIACAO
      FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDOS_PGOS_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.DATA_PAGTO,
               (CALCULATED TIPO_APROVACAO);
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 264 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_RESULTADO_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.ASSESSORIA_CORR,
          t1.CHAVE,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS COUNT_of_CHAVE
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL t1
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.ASSESSORIA_CORR,
               t1.CHAVE
      ORDER BY COUNT_of_CHAVE DESC;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 267 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 268 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA PERF_CA;
SET CARTAO.PERF_AMIGAVEL;
where ASSESSORIA <> 'PILOTO_BOM';
IF DU_REGULARIZADO ='.' THEN DU_REGULARIZADO = 1;
KEEP DU_REGULARIZADO FX_ATRASO_NEW SAFRA VLR_COBRANCA VLR_REGULARIZADO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE PERF_CA AS
SELECT
distinct
SAFRA,
FX_ATRASO_NEW,
DU_REGULARIZADO,
SUM(VLR_COBRANCA) AS RISCO,
SUM(VLR_REGULARIZADO) AS REGULARIZADO
FROM PERF_CA
GROUP BY
SAFRA,
FX_ATRASO_NEW,
DU_REGULARIZADO
;
QUIT;
proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA   ;
run;

proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW   ;
run;

proc sort data=PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW DU_REGULARIZADO;
run;


data PERF_CA_ACUM;
    set PERF_CA;
    by SAFRA FX_ATRASO_NEW;

    retain risco_acum regularizado_acum 0;

    if first.FX_ATRASO_NEW then do;
        risco_acum = 0;
        regularizado_acum = 0;
    end;

    risco_acum + RISCO;
    regularizado_acum + REGULARIZADO;
run;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.resultado_ca AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.FX_ATRASO_NEW,
          t1.DU_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (t1.regularizado_acum/t1.risco_acum) AS perf
      FROM WORK.PERF_CA_ACUM t1
      WHERE t1.SAFRA >= '1Oct2024'd;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_PERF_CL AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.FX_ATRASO,
          t1.DU_DT_REF,
          
            (SUM(t1.SALDO_ENVIADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_SALDO_ENVIADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PARCELADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_PARCELADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_QUITADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_QUITADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV_PARCELADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_BV_PARCELADO,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV_QUITADO)) FORMAT=BESTX15.2 AS SUM_of_RECEBIDO_BV_QUITADO
      FROM WORK.PERF_CL t1
      WHERE t1.DATA_BASE >= 202410
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.FX_ATRASO,
               t1.DU_DT_REF;
QUIT;

          t1.FX_ATRASO,

SELECT
A.*,
CASE
WHEN A.PGTO_AGO IS MISSING AND A.PGTO_SET IS MISSING AND A.FAIXA_SET IS MISSING AND A.STATUS_ENTRADA IS MISSING
THEN 'VALIDAR'
ELSE 'OK'
END AS STATUS_FINAL

FROM VALIDA_CRC_AGO25 A
;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.base_recebimento_cl AS
   SELECT t1.DATA_BASE,
          t1.STATUS,
          t1.CL_STATUS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VR_SALDO_ATUAL)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS VR_SALDO_ATUAL,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_BV)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS RECEBIDO_BV,
          
            (SUM(t1.RECEBIDO_PERF)) FORMAT=BESTx15.2 AS RECEBIDO_PERF,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CONTRATO)) AS QTD_CONTRATOS
      FROM WORK.RESULTADO_CL t1
      WHERE t1.TIPO NOT = 'COLCHAO' AND t1.DATA_BASE = 202510
      GROUP BY t1.DATA_BASE,
               t1.STATUS,
               t1.CL_STATUS,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */

%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.BASE_ACOMPANHAMENTO_CA_CAROL);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACOMPANHAMENTO_CA_CAROL AS
   SELECT t1.SAFRA,
          t1.COD_ASSESS,
          t1.ASSESSORIA,
          
            (MAX(t1.DU_REGULARIZADO)) FORMAT=BEST12. AS ULT_DU_REG,
          
            (COUNT(t1.CHAVE)) AS QTD_CONTRATOS,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_COBRANCA)) FORMAT=bestx15.2 AS VLR_COBRANCA,
          
            (SUM(t1.VLR_REGULARIZADO)) FORMAT=bestx15.2 AS VLR_REGULARIZADO,
          
            (SUM(case
            when t1.PILOTO_DIGITAL ='N' then t1.VLR_COBRANCA else 0 end)) FORMAT=bestx15.2 AS RISCO_SEM_PILOTO
      FROM WORK.PERF_AMIGAVEL t1
      GROUP BY t1.SAFRA,
               t1.COD_ASSESS,
               t1.ASSESSORIA;
QUIT;
